   612	  /**
   613	   * reserveFiles — D-163 §2. Persists the `DocumentFile` set for a `DRAFT` Version in a
   614	   * single `TransactWriteItems`: the Update on `DocumentVersion` (`fileSetSealed`/
   615	   * `principalFileId`/`totalFiles`/`pendingFileScans`) is what actually closes the race
   616	   * between two concurrent calls each supplying a single PRINCIPAL with distinct `fileId`s
   617	   * (input validation alone — `assertExactlyOnePrincipal` — cannot close that race, only the
   618	   * transaction's own condition on `fileSetSealed` can, since it's the one write both callers
   619	   * necessarily contend on). Only permitted while the Version is `DRAFT` and not yet sealed.
   620	   *
   621	   * Item 3 (2026-09-02): presigns each file's upload URL against its own `quarantineObject`
   622	   * key AFTER the transaction commits — mirrors M6's `document-service.ts` ordering (DynamoDB
   623	   * admission point gates a new presigned URL, never the other way around). A presign failure
   624	   * here therefore never leaves an orphaned `DocumentFile` row with no way to ever be uploaded
   625	   * to: the row already exists and is retried by the reconciliation worker's TIMEOUT path if
   626	   * the caller can't retry the presign directly.
   627	   */
   628	  async reserveFiles(ctx: RequestContext, documentId: string, seq: number, expectedVersion: number, files: readonly FileUploadSpec[]): Promise<ReservedFile[]> {
   629	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:upload", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   630	    assertExactlyOnePrincipal(files);
   631	    if (files.length > MAX_FILES_PER_VERSION) {
   632	      throw new ValidationError(`At most ${MAX_FILES_PER_VERSION} files per DocumentVersion.`, { documentId, seq, count: files.length });
   633	    }
   634	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   635	    const key = documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, seq);
   636	    const current = await this.store.get<DocumentVersion>(key);
   637	    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError("DocumentVersion not found.", { documentId, seq });
   638	    if (current.state !== "DRAFT") throw new ConflictError("Files can only be reserved while the DocumentVersion is DRAFT.", { documentId, seq, state: current.state });
   639	    if (current.fileSetSealed) throw new ConflictError("This DocumentVersion's file set is already sealed.", { documentId, seq });
   640	
   641	    const now = this.now();
   642	    const principalSpec = files.find((f) => f.role === "PRINCIPAL");
   643	    if (!principalSpec) throw new ValidationError("Exactly one PRINCIPAL is required.", { documentId, seq });
   644	
   645	    // storage-quota-scoping (2026-09-09, D-2xx): reservation-time capacity hold, checked and
   646	    // incremented in the SAME transaction that seals the file set below — closes the
   647	    // oversubscription gap Codex's independent proposal caught (N concurrent reservations
   648	    // against a near-full quota reading the same stale `usedBytes` and all passing). The OCC
   649	    // condition on the quota row's `version` is what actually serializes concurrent batches;
   650	    // this in-memory check only produces a clear, early `QuotaExceededError` message.
   651	    const requestedBytes = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.contentLength, 0);
   652	    const quota = await this.ensureStorageQuota(tenantId);
   653	    if (wouldExceedStorageQuota(quota, requestedBytes)) {
   654	      throw new QuotaExceededError("Tenant storage quota would be exceeded by this upload.", {
   655	        tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId,
   656	        limitBytes: quota.limitBytes,
   657	        usedBytes: quota.usedBytes,
   658	        reservedBytes: quota.reservedBytes,
   659	        requestedBytes,
   660	      });
   661	    }
   662	
   663	    const documentFiles: DocumentFile[] = files.map((spec) => {
   664	      const fileId = this.ids.newFileId();
   665	      // D-179 slice 3: the scan-timeout deadline is fully known right here (createdAt=now, the
   666	      // file starts PENDING_UPLOAD), so the GSI8 MaintenanceDueIndex pointer is stamped in this
   667	      // same Put — same "write the pointer at the real transition, and creation IS that
   668	      // transition when the due date needs no later event" reasoning invitationGsi8Keys() uses
   669	      // for a PENDING Invitation.
   670	      const due = deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue({ scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD", createdAt: now })!;
   671	      return {
   672	        ...documentFileKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, fileId),
   673	        entityType: "DocumentFile",
   674	        tenantId,
   675	        documentId,
   676	        versionId: current.versionId,
   677	        seq,
   678	        fileId,
   679	        role: spec.role,
   680	        scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD",
   681	        mediaType: spec.mediaType,
   682	        contentLength: spec.contentLength,
   683	        checksumSha256: spec.checksumSha256,
   684	        // D-163 §1: versionId consolidated later, atomically, by whichever physical event
   685	        // (S3 Object Created / GuardDuty finding) observes the real object first — never
   686	        // fabricated here (same placeholder discipline M6's Document.quarantineObject uses).
   687	        quarantineObject: { bucket: this.quarantineBucket, key: this.buildQuarantineKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, fileId), versionId: "" },
   688	        createdAt: now,
   689	        updatedAt: now,
   690	        version: 1,
   691	        ...documentFileGsi8Keys({ dueAtIso: due.dueAtIso, tenantId, fileId }),
   692	      };
   693	    });
   694	    const principalFileId = documentFiles.find((f) => f.role === "PRINCIPAL")!.fileId;
   695	
   696	    const entries = [
   697	      {
   698	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   699	          tableName: this.tableName,
   700	          key,
   701	          tenantId,
   702	          expectedVersion,
   703	          set: { fileSetSealed: true, principalFileId, totalFiles: documentFiles.length, pendingFileScans: documentFiles.length },
   704	          now,
   705	          extraConditions: [
   706	            { expression: "#st = :draft", names: { "#st": "state" }, values: { ":draft": "DRAFT" } },
   707	            { expression: "attribute_not_exists(#sealed) OR #sealed = :false", names: { "#sealed": "fileSetSealed" }, values: { ":false": false } },
   708	          ],
   709	        }),
   710	      },
   711	      ...documentFiles.map((file) => ({ Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, file as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) })),
   712	      {
   713	        // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): the transactional re-assertion of the same check above
   714	        // — the ONE write that actually serializes concurrent reservations against the shared
   715	        // quota row (the earlier in-memory check only gives a fast, clear error message).
   716	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   717	          tableName: this.tableName,
   718	          key: storageQuotaKey(tenantId),
   719	          tenantId,
   720	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   721	          set: { reservedBytes: quota.reservedBytes + requestedBytes },
   722	          now,
   723	          extraConditions: [
   724	            {
   725	              expression: "#used + #reserved + :requested <= #limit",
   726	              names: { "#used": "usedBytes", "#reserved": "reservedBytes", "#limit": "limitBytes" },
   727	              values: { ":requested": requestedBytes },
   728	            },
   729	          ],
   730	        }),
   731	      },
   732	    ];
   733	    try {
   734	      await this.store.transactWrite(entries);
   735	    } catch (err) {
   736	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) throw new ConflictError("DocumentVersion's file set was concurrently reserved, the Version is no longer DRAFT, or the tenant's storage quota was concurrently exhausted — retry.", { documentId, seq });
   737	      throw err;
   738	    }
   739	
   740	    return Promise.all(
   741	      documentFiles.map(async (file) => {
   742	        const presigned = await this.signer.presignUpload({
   743	          bucket: file.quarantineObject.bucket,
   744	          key: file.quarantineObject.key,
   745	          mediaType: file.mediaType,
   746	          contentLength: file.contentLength,
   747	          checksumSha256: file.checksumSha256,
   748	          metadata: { documentId, versionId: current.versionId, fileId: file.fileId, tenantId },
   749	          expiresInSeconds: PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS,
   750	        });
   751	        return { file, uploadUrl: presigned.uploadUrl, requiredHeaders: presigned.requiredHeaders };
   752	      }),
   753	    );
   754	  }
   755	
   756	  /** D-163 §7: mirrors M6's quarantine key convention (`document-service.ts`'s
   757	   * `tenant/<t>/item/<i>/document/<d>/slot/<s>/<random>`), namespaced under `document-archive/`
   758	   * so the two key formats coexist in the same physical bucket without ever colliding — the S3
   759	   * event handler routes on this prefix to pick the right parser (D-163 §7, deferred). Never
   760	   * encodes the original file name (PII) — only internal identifiers. */
   761	  private buildQuarantineKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, documentId: string, seq: number, fileId: string): string {
   762	    return `document-archive/tenant/${tenantId}/document/${documentId}/version/${seq}/file/${fileId}`;
   763	  }
   764	
   765	  /** Get-or-create of the default storage quota row — same "auto-provisioning" shape
   766	   * `SubjectService.ensureEntitlement()` established (D-038's `TenantQuotaService` precedent),
   767	   * ported here rather than reused directly because `document-archive` cannot import from
   768	   * `subject/**` (`.dependency-cruiser.cjs`; see `storage-quota.ts`'s module doc comment). */
   769	  private async ensureStorageQuota(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId): Promise<TenantStorageQuota> {
   770	    const key = storageQuotaKey(tenantId);
   771	    const existing = await this.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
   772	    if (existing) return existing;
   773	    const created = defaultStorageQuota(tenantId, this.now());
   774	    const wrote = await this.store.putIfAbsent(created);
   775	    if (wrote) return created;
   776	    // Lost the creation race — another concurrent call already created it; re-read real state.
   777	    const fresh = await this.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
   778	    if (!fresh) throw new ConflictError("Storage quota record vanished after creation race.", { tenantId });
   779	    return fresh;
   780	  }
   781	
   782	  /** storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): the read route this exposes reuses `docarchive:read`
   783	   * (`READ_ONLY_ROLES`) — same tier as every other tenant-wide summary this module already
   784	   * serves (`listReviewQueue`, D-248), by direct analogy: a byte-count aggregate carries no more
   785	   * sensitivity than review-queue membership, and every role that can upload should be able to
   786	   * see why an upload might be refused. */
   787	  async getStorageQuotaUsage(ctx: RequestContext): Promise<StorageQuotaUsage> {
   788	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:read", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   789	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   790	    const quota = await this.ensureStorageQuota(tenantId);
   791	    return projectStorageQuotaUsage(quota);
   792	  }
   793	
   794	  /** DRAFT -> RECEIVED. File presence/malware-scan-clean validation is the caller's
   795	   * responsibility in this increment (DocumentFile persistence is a follow-up slice) — this
   796	   * method only enforces the state-machine transition itself.
   797	   *
   798	   * D-163 §4 gate, activated now that `reserveFiles()` has a real HTTP route (D-167): a Version
   799	   * cannot commit until its file set is sealed (`reserveFiles()` ran and produced a determined,
   800	   * immutable set of files), closing the race where a caller commits while files are still being
   801	   * added/never reserved at all. The precondition is enforced twice — an in-memory pre-check for
   802	   * a clear `ConflictError` message, and a transactional `ConditionCheck` (TOCTOU-safe, same
   803	   * pattern as the PRINCIPAL fence in `acceptVersion()`, D-163 §5) so a concurrent `reserveFiles()`
   804	   * that seals the set between the read and the write can never be missed. */
   805	  async commitUpload(ctx: RequestContext, documentId: string, seq: number, expectedVersion: number): Promise<DocumentVersion> {
   806	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:upload", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   807	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   808	    const key = documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, seq);
   809	    const current = await this.store.get<DocumentVersion>(key);
   810	    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError("DocumentVersion not found.", { documentId, seq });
     1	/**
     2	 * DocumentFile — D-163 (`docs/architecture/reviews/document-file-scoping/estado-final-

     6	 * DocumentVersion, keyed by versionId" — reuses the same evidence/object-reference types

    26	export type DocumentFileScanStatus = "PENDING_UPLOAD" | "SCANNING" | "CLEAN" | "REJECTED" | "UNSUPPORTED" | "TIMEOUT";

    28	/** Non-terminal states — a file in one of these can still be advanced by a physical event
    29	 * (S3 Object Created / GuardDuty finding) or by reconciliation timeout. */
    30	const NON_TERMINAL_SCAN_STATUSES: readonly DocumentFileScanStatus[] = ["PENDING_UPLOAD", "SCANNING"];

    32	export function isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(status: DocumentFileScanStatus): boolean {
    33	  return NON_TERMINAL_SCAN_STATUSES.includes(status);

    36	export interface DocumentFile extends EntityKey {
    37	  entityType: "DocumentFile";
    38	  tenantId: string;
    39	  documentId: string;
    40	  versionId: string;
    41	  seq: number;
    42	  fileId: string;
    43	  role: DocumentFileRole;
    44	  scanStatus: DocumentFileScanStatus;
    45	  mediaType: string;
    46	  contentLength: number;
    47	  checksumSha256: string;

    49	  cleanObject?: DocumentObjectReference;
    50	  uploadEvidence?: UploadEvidence;
    51	  malwareEvidence?: MalwareEvidence;
    52	  createdAt: string;
    53	  updatedAt: string;
    54	  version: number;
    55	  /** MaintenanceDueIndex pointer (D-179 slice 3/D-166 follow-up) — present only while
    56	   * `scanStatus` is non-terminal, removed atomically by the same transaction that reaches a
    57	   * terminal state. REPLACES the GSI5-based `fileReconciliationGsi5Keys()` pointer D-164/D-166
    58	   * defined: that mechanism was discovered, on re-reading the write path before this migration,
    59	   * to have never had a real writer (`reserveFiles()` never called it) — the reconciliation
    60	   * worker's Scan filtered on `attribute_exists(GSI5PK)` could therefore never find a real
    61	   * candidate in production. GSI8 is a clean replacement, not a second mechanism alongside a
    62	   * working one — same D-179 mandate the other 8 workers follow. */
    63	  GSI8PK?: string;
    64	  GSI8SK?: string;

    67	export function documentFileKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, documentId: string, seq: number, fileId: string): EntityKey {
    68	  return { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#DOCUMENT#${documentId}`, SK: `VERSION#${formatVersionSeq(seq)}#FILE#${fileId}` };
    69	}
    70	
    71	/** Upload+scan window (D-179 slice 3) — mirrors the presign URL's own TTL
    72	 * (`document-archive-service.ts`'s `PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS`, which imports this constant rather
    73	 * than duplicating it): the whole reserve->scan lifecycle is bounded by the same window the
    74	 * presigned upload URL itself expires under, so a file still non-terminal past this point can no
    75	 * longer even be uploaded through the URL it was issued, and TIMEOUT is the right verdict. */
    76	export const FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 600;
    77	const MS_PER_SECOND = 1000;
    78	
    79	/** GSI8 (MaintenanceDueIndex, D-179) namespace this worker owns — the ONLY value any
    80	 * document-file-reconciliation-scoped IAM policy's `dynamodb:LeadingKeys` condition may
    81	 * reference (`infra/modules/dynamo-table/main.tf`), alongside the DLQ counterpart. */
    82	export const DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION_WORK_TYPE = "DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION";
    83	
    84	export interface MaintenanceDue {
    85	  dueAtIso: string;
    86	}
    87	
    88	/**
    89	 * deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue — D-179 slice 3's pure due-date function, same role as
    90	 * `deriveMembershipMaintenanceDue()`/`deriveInvitationMaintenanceDue()`: single source of truth
    91	 * reused by the write path (`reserveFiles()`), the backfill script, and the worker's own
    92	 * processing (via `applyFileScanTimeout`'s fresh re-read).
    93	 *
    94	 * Closer to Invitation's PENDING branch than to Membership's REMOVED branch: the due date is
    95	 * fully known at creation (`reserveFiles()`) — `createdAt + FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` — there is
    96	 * no later transition ("scan started") to hang it on, since SCANNING is just evidence arriving
    97	 * within the same fixed window, never a new clock start. `undefined` once terminal (never a real
    98	 * candidate again).
    99	 */
   100	export function deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue(file: Pick<DocumentFile, "scanStatus" | "createdAt">): MaintenanceDue | undefined {
   101	  if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return undefined;
   102	  return { dueAtIso: new Date(Date.parse(file.createdAt) + FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS * MS_PER_SECOND).toISOString() };
   103	}
   104	
   105	/** `GSI8PK=WORK#DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION` / `GSI8SK=<dueAtIso>#TENANT#<tenantId>#<fileId>`
   106	 * (D-179's exact key spec, same shape as `membershipGsi8Keys()`/`invitationGsi8Keys()`) —
   107	 * `documentId`/`seq` are not embedded here since a `KEYS_ONLY` GSI8 Query already returns the
   108	 * base table's own `PK`/`SK`, which already encode them (`documentFileKey()`). */
   109	export function documentFileGsi8Keys(input: { dueAtIso: string; tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId; fileId: string }): { GSI8PK: string; GSI8SK: string } {
   110	  return {
   111	    GSI8PK: `WORK#${DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION_WORK_TYPE}`,
   112	    GSI8SK: `${input.dueAtIso}#TENANT#${input.tenantId}#${input.fileId}`,
   113	  };
   114	}

   123	export class InvalidFileSetError extends Error {
   124	  constructor(reason: string) {
   125	    super(`Invalid file set for reserveFiles: ${reason}`);
   126	    this.name = "InvalidFileSetError";
   127	  }
   128	}
   129	
   130	/** D-143 Decision 6: exactly one PRINCIPAL per batch. Pure validation, checked before any
   131	 * write is attempted — the actual concurrency race between two batches reserving the same
   132	 * DocumentVersion is closed separately, by the transaction's `fileSetSealed` fence in
   133	 * `DocumentArchiveService.reserveFiles()` (D-163 §2), never by this check alone. */
   134	export function assertExactlyOnePrincipal(files: readonly FileUploadSpec[]): void {
   135	  if (files.length === 0) throw new InvalidFileSetError("at least one file is required");
   136	  const principals = files.filter((f) => f.role === "PRINCIPAL");
   137	  if (principals.length !== 1) throw new InvalidFileSetError(`exactly one PRINCIPAL is required, got ${principals.length}`);
   138	}

   140	/** D-163 §2: caps a single `reserveFiles` batch well under `TransactWriteItems`'s 100-action
   141	 * limit (N Put + 1 Update = N+1 actions in the worst case). */
   142	export const MAX_FILES_PER_VERSION = 20;
     1	/**
     2	 * DocumentRequest — document-archive's OWN minimal shape, D-143 Decision 4 (guest access). NOT
     3	 * the older `src/modules/subject/domain/document-request.ts` (that entity belongs to the
     4	 * subject module's M10 guest-upload slice, keyed under `TENANT#t#SUBJECT#s`/
     5	 * `REQASSIGN#<assignmentId>#DOCREQ#<id>` and carrying its own `tokenSelectorHash` inline) — this
     6	 * is a distinct, new entity scoped to the document-archive domain (Document/DocumentVersion/
     7	 * Requirement), the one `estado-final-consolidado.md` Decision 8 (recurrence) references via
     8	 * `requestId`/`attemptIndex`/`seriesId`.
     9	 *
    10	 * Deliberately minimal: this task (guest access, D-143 Decision 4) only needs "the business
    11	 * request a RequestAccessCredential is issued against" — id/subjectId/tenantId/requirementId/
    12	 * status/deadline. Recurrence (Decision 8, D-147) ADDS `seriesId`/`occurrenceId`/
    13	 * `attemptIndex`/`parentRequestId` below rather than introducing a parallel entity.
    14	 *
    15	 * D-147 (Decision 8, recurrence): all four new fields are OPTIONAL/additive — a bare
    16	 * `DocumentRequest` created outside a series (the only kind guest access's existing tests
    17	 * construct) simply omits them, same "no fabricated value" discipline as every other optional
    18	 * field in this module (`requirement.ts`'s `evidenceValidUntil`, etc.). `attemptIndex` is the
    19	 * one exception worth calling out: it defaults to `1` for a non-recurring request (see
    20	 * `document-request-series.ts`'s `materializeAttempt`), so it is typed as a required `number`
    21	 * with `1` as the implicit non-recurring value, not `attemptIndex?: number` — every
    22	 * DocumentRequest that exists IS exactly one attempt, whether or not it belongs to a series.
    23	 */
    24	import type { EntityKey } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/occ.js";
    25	import type { AuthorizedTenantId } from "../../identity/domain/authorization.js";
    26	
    27	/** Mirrors the older subject-module DocumentRequest's status vocabulary (same states cover the
    28	 * same real lifecycle — requested/opened/submitted/completed, plus the three ways it can die
    29	 * early) — not reused by import (distinct module, distinct entity), just the same taxonomy. */
    30	export type DocumentRequestStatus = "REQUESTED" | "OPENED" | "SUBMITTED" | "COMPLETED" | "CANCELLED" | "EXPIRED" | "REVOKED";
    31	
    32	export interface DocumentRequest extends EntityKey {
    33	  SK: `DOCREQUEST#${string}`;
    34	  entityType: "DocumentRequest";
    35	  documentRequestId: string;
    36	  tenantId: string;
    37	  subjectId: string;
    38	  /** The Requirement this request asks the guest to satisfy — a request always targets exactly
    39	   * one Requirement in this minimal shape (no fan-out to multiple Requirements per request). */
    40	  requirementId: string;
    41	  status: DocumentRequestStatus;
    42	  /** Also the RequestAccessCredential's TTL (Decision 4: "TTL = prazo do Request de negócio") —
    43	   * absent means no deadline, in which case credential issuance must supply an explicit TTL of
    44	   * its own rather than defaulting to "forever" (see `issueRequestAccessCredential`). */
    45	  deadline?: string;
    46	  lastOpenedAt?: string;
    47	  lastSubmissionId?: string;
    48	  submissionCount: number;
    49	  /** D-147 (Decision 8): present only when this request belongs to a `DocumentRequestSeries`
    50	   * cycle — see `document-request-series.ts` for the series/cycle/attempt shape. */
    51	  seriesId?: string;
    52	  /** Deterministic per-cycle id (`computeSeriesOccurrenceId`) — stable across every attempt of
    53	   * the SAME cycle, changes only when the series advances to its next cycle. Never present
    54	   * without `seriesId`. */
    55	  occurrenceId?: string;
    56	  /** Which attempt within the cycle this is — `1` for a series' first attempt of a cycle,
    57	   * incrementing on each `materializeAttempt` call within the same cycle. Optional (not
    58	   * defaulted to `1`) rather than required: a bare, non-recurring `DocumentRequest` (guest
    59	   * access's existing shape, no `seriesId`) has no cycle at all, so "attempt 1 of what?" does
    60	   * not apply to it — forcing a fabricated `1` onto every non-recurring request would violate
    61	   * this module's "no fabricated value" discipline (see `requirement.ts`'s `evidenceValidUntil`
    62	   * for the same principle applied elsewhere). Always present together with `seriesId`. */
    63	  attemptIndex?: number;
    64	  /** Always the immediately-previous attempt of the SAME cycle (never the previous cycle's
    65	   * last attempt) — preserves causality within a cycle while `occurrenceId` preserves cycle
    66	   * identity across attempts (D-147/Decision 8). Absent for attempt 1 of any cycle. */
    67	  parentRequestId?: string;
    68	  /**
    69	   * D-226 (`guest-credential-issuance-scoping/estado-final-consolidado.md`): monotonic counter,
    70	   * starts at `1` at creation, incremented by `rejectVersion()` on every reissue-triggering
    71	   * rejection (Achado 3 of D-222). Identity for credential ISSUANCE is `documentRequestId` +
    72	   * `issuanceGeneration` together, never `documentRequestId` alone — this is what lets a
    73	   * post-rejection reissue avoid colliding with the original issuance's idempotency record on
    74	   * the guest Lambda's consumer, and what the consumer's `DocumentRequest` `Update` fences on
    75	   * (`ConditionExpression` includes `issuanceGeneration = :expected`) to reject a stale/obsolete
    76	   * issuance event without a TOCTOU window.
    77	   */
    78	  issuanceGeneration: number;
    79	  /**
    80	   * D-226: selector hash of the currently-active `RequestAccessCredential` for this request —
    81	   * written ONLY by the guest-Lambda issuance consumer (the one holding the D-146 pepper), never
    82	   * by this (authenticated, pepper-less) Lambda. Its purpose is to let THIS Lambda revoke a
    83	   * stale credential (`rejectVersion()`) by a known key, without ever needing to compute or
    84	   * verify a hash itself — revocation is a plain conditional `SET revokedAt`, no pepper required.
    85	   */
    86	  activeCredentialSelectorHash?: string;
    87	  /**
    88	   * D-226 (Achado 3 of D-222): set by `rejectVersion()` when it reopens this request — one
    89	   * object, never two loose fields, so the reason always stays correlated to the exact version
    90	   * and moment it was rejected (never an ambiguous "last reason" disconnected from which
    91	   * submission it belonged to).
    92	   */
    93	  lastRejection?: { versionId: string; reason: string; occurredAt: string };
    94	  /**
    95	   * D-228 (closes D-222/D-227's named gap: this entity had no way to know WHO to deliver a
    96	   * guest link to). Optional/additive — same "no fabricated value" discipline as every other
    97	   * optional field here — following the exact precedent already established for the SAME
    98	   * problem (delivering a guest link to an external party) in the older, unrelated
    99	   * `src/modules/subject/domain/document-request.ts`'s `recipientEmail: string` (there
   100	   * required, because that entity's ONLY creation path — `CreateDocumentRequestInput` —
   101	   * already demands it; here optional, because a series-materialized attempt
   102	   * (`document-request-series.ts`'s `materializeAttempt`) has no recipient contact modeled at
   103	   * the series level yet — a genuine product gap named, not solved, by this decision; see
   104	   * decisions-log.md D-228). Populated only by `createDocumentRequest()`'s avulso path today.
   105	   * The delivery worker (`src/workers/guest-credential-delivery/deliver.ts`) treats its
   106	   * absence as a terminal, non-retryable skip rather than an error — a request with no
   107	   * recipient is a data gap, not a transient failure.
   108	   */
   109	  recipientEmail?: string;
   110	  createdAt: string;
   111	  updatedAt: string;
   112	  version: number;
   113	}
   114	
   115	export function documentRequestKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, subjectId: string, documentRequestId: string): { PK: string; SK: `DOCREQUEST#${string}` } {
   116	  return { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#SUBJECT#${subjectId}`, SK: `DOCREQUEST#${documentRequestId}` };
   117	}

   121	/** States a resolved credential/session may still act against — mirrors
   122	 * `GuestSubmissionService.resolveToken`'s terminal-status rejection list (subject module
   123	 * precedent) applied to this module's own status vocabulary. */
   124	export function isDocumentRequestLive(status: DocumentRequestStatus): boolean {
   125	  return status !== "CANCELLED" && status !== "REVOKED" && status !== "EXPIRED" && status !== "COMPLETED";

    53	interface GuardDutyScanResultDetail {
    54	  s3ObjectDetails: { bucketName: string; objectKey: string; versionId?: string };
    55	  scanResultDetails: { scanResultStatus: string };
    56	  scanStatus: string;
    57	}
    58	
    59	export async function handler(event: SQSEvent): Promise<SQSBatchResponse> {
    60	  const batchItemFailures: { itemIdentifier: string }[] = [];
    61	
    62	  for (const record of event.Records) {
    63	    await runWithContext({ correlationId: randomUUID() }, async () => {
    64	      try {
    65	        const message = JSON.parse(record.body) as { detail?: GuardDutyScanResultDetail; id?: string };
    66	        const detail = message.detail;
    67	        if (!detail?.s3ObjectDetails?.bucketName || !detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey) {
    68	          logger.error("malware-result malformed GuardDuty event", { messageId: record.messageId });
    69	          batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.messageId });
    70	          return;
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        const status = detail.scanResultDetails?.scanResultStatus;
    74	        if (!status || !REAL_STATUSES.has(status as GuardDutyScanStatus)) {
    75	          logger.error("malware-result unrecognized scan status", { status });
    76	          batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.messageId });
    77	          return;
    78	        }
    79	
    80	        // D-193 slice 1: tried FIRST, same "never collides" reasoning as upload-finalizer-
    81	        // handler.ts's own comment on this branch.
    82	        const parsedArchive = parseDocumentArchiveQuarantineKey(detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey);
    83	        if (parsedArchive) {
    84	          await runWithContext({ correlationId: randomUUID(), tenantId: parsedArchive.tenantId }, async () => {
    85	            const outcome = await processDocumentArchiveMalwareResult(documentArchiveDeps, {
    86	              // `parsedArchive.tenantId` is parsed out of the S3 object key — a physical key
    87	              // our own upload-reservation code wrote from an authorized tenantId at reserve
    88	              // time, never a client-controlled string. Same one-hop-removed provenance as an
    89	              // SQS wake-up hint.
    90	              tenantId: authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(parsedArchive),
    91	              documentId: parsedArchive.documentId,
    92	              seq: parsedArchive.seq,
    93	              fileId: parsedArchive.fileId,
    94	              object: { bucket: detail.s3ObjectDetails.bucketName, key: detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey, versionId: detail.s3ObjectDetails.versionId ?? "" },
    95	              status: status as GuardDutyScanStatus,
    96	              scanResultId: message.id ?? record.messageId,
    97	            });
    98	            logger.info("malware-result document-archive outcome", { documentId: parsedArchive.documentId, fileId: parsedArchive.fileId, outcome, status });
    99	          });
   100	          return;
   101	        }
   102	
   103	        const parsed = parseQuarantineKey(detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey);
   104	        if (parsed) {
   105	          await runWithContext({ correlationId: randomUUID(), tenantId: parsed.tenantId }, async () => {
   106	            const outcome = await processMalwareResult(deps, {
   107	              tenantId: parsed.tenantId,
   108	              itemId: parsed.itemId,
   109	              documentId: parsed.documentId,
   110	              object: { bucket: detail.s3ObjectDetails.bucketName, key: detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey, versionId: detail.s3ObjectDetails.versionId ?? "" },
   111	              status: status as GuardDutyScanStatus,
   112	              scanResultId: message.id ?? record.messageId,
   113	            });
   114	            logger.info("malware-result outcome", { documentId: parsed.documentId, outcome, status });
   115	          });
   116	          return;
   117	        }
   118	
   119	        const parsedSubmission = parseSubmissionQuarantineKey(detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey);
   120	        if (!parsedSubmission) {
   121	          logger.error("malware-result unrecognized key shape", { key: detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey });
   122	          return;
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        await runWithContext({ correlationId: randomUUID(), tenantId: parsedSubmission.tenantId }, async () => {
   126	          const outcome = await processSubmissionMalwareResult(submissionDeps, {
   127	            // Same S3-object-key provenance as the document-archive branch above -
   128	            // parsed from a key our own upload-reservation code wrote, not client input.
   129	            tenantId: authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(parsedSubmission),
   130	            subjectId: parsedSubmission.subjectId,
   131	            assignmentId: parsedSubmission.assignmentId,
   132	            submissionId: parsedSubmission.submissionId,
   133	            object: { bucket: detail.s3ObjectDetails.bucketName, key: detail.s3ObjectDetails.objectKey, versionId: detail.s3ObjectDetails.versionId ?? "" },
   134	            status: status as GuardDutyScanStatus,
   135	            scanResultId: message.id ?? record.messageId,
   136	          });
   137	          logger.info("malware-result submission outcome", { submissionId: parsedSubmission.submissionId, outcome, status });
   138	        });
   139	      } catch (err) {
   140	        // Real incident (2026-08-22): a bare `err.message` of "UnknownError" (AWS SDK v3's
   141	        // fallback for a response it couldn't classify) gave zero diagnostic signal for a real
   142	        // production-blocking promotion failure. Logging name/code/$metadata too - none of
   143	        // these are secrets (HTTP status, request id, AWS error code), safe for SecureLogger.
   144	        const e = err as { name?: string; message?: string; Code?: string; $metadata?: unknown } | undefined;
   145	        logger.error("malware-result failed", {
   146	          messageId: record.messageId,
   147	          error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
   148	          errorName: e?.name,
   149	          errorCode: e?.Code,
   150	          errorMetadata: e?.$metadata,
   151	        });
   152	        batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.messageId });
   153	      }
   154	    });
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  return { batchItemFailures };
   158	}

   756	  /** D-163 §7: mirrors M6's quarantine key convention (`document-service.ts`'s
   757	   * `tenant/<t>/item/<i>/document/<d>/slot/<s>/<random>`), namespaced under `document-archive/`
   758	   * so the two key formats coexist in the same physical bucket without ever colliding — the S3
   759	   * event handler routes on this prefix to pick the right parser (D-163 §7, deferred). Never
   760	   * encodes the original file name (PII) — only internal identifiers. */
   761	  private buildQuarantineKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, documentId: string, seq: number, fileId: string): string {
   762	    return `document-archive/tenant/${tenantId}/document/${documentId}/version/${seq}/file/${fileId}`;

   764	
   765	  /** Get-or-create of the default storage quota row — same "auto-provisioning" shape
   766	   * `SubjectService.ensureEntitlement()` established (D-038's `TenantQuotaService` precedent),
   767	   * ported here rather than reused directly because `document-archive` cannot import from
   768	   * `subject/**` (`.dependency-cruiser.cjs`; see `storage-quota.ts`'s module doc comment). */
   769	  private async ensureStorageQuota(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId): Promise<TenantStorageQuota> {
   770	    const key = storageQuotaKey(tenantId);
   771	    const existing = await this.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
   772	    if (existing) return existing;
   773	    const created = defaultStorageQuota(tenantId, this.now());
   774	    const wrote = await this.store.putIfAbsent(created);
   775	    if (wrote) return created;
   776	    // Lost the creation race — another concurrent call already created it; re-read real state.
   777	    const fresh = await this.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
   778	    if (!fresh) throw new ConflictError("Storage quota record vanished after creation race.", { tenantId });
   779	    return fresh;
   780	  }

    67	export function documentFileKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, documentId: string, seq: number, fileId: string): EntityKey {
    68	  return { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#DOCUMENT#${documentId}`, SK: `VERSION#${formatVersionSeq(seq)}#FILE#${fileId}` };
    69	}
    70	
    71	/** Upload+scan window (D-179 slice 3) — mirrors the presign URL's own TTL
    72	 * (`document-archive-service.ts`'s `PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS`, which imports this constant rather
    73	 * than duplicating it): the whole reserve->scan lifecycle is bounded by the same window the
    74	 * presigned upload URL itself expires under, so a file still non-terminal past this point can no
    75	 * longer even be uploaded through the URL it was issued, and TIMEOUT is the right verdict. */
    76	export const FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 600;
    77	const MS_PER_SECOND = 1000;
    78	
    79	/** GSI8 (MaintenanceDueIndex, D-179) namespace this worker owns — the ONLY value any
    80	 * document-file-reconciliation-scoped IAM policy's `dynamodb:LeadingKeys` condition may
    81	 * reference (`infra/modules/dynamo-table/main.tf`), alongside the DLQ counterpart. */
    82	export const DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION_WORK_TYPE = "DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION";
    83	
    84	export interface MaintenanceDue {
    85	  dueAtIso: string;
    86	}
    87	
    88	/**
    89	 * deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue — D-179 slice 3's pure due-date function, same role as
    90	 * `deriveMembershipMaintenanceDue()`/`deriveInvitationMaintenanceDue()`: single source of truth
    91	 * reused by the write path (`reserveFiles()`), the backfill script, and the worker's own
    92	 * processing (via `applyFileScanTimeout`'s fresh re-read).
    93	 *
    94	 * Closer to Invitation's PENDING branch than to Membership's REMOVED branch: the due date is
    95	 * fully known at creation (`reserveFiles()`) — `createdAt + FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` — there is
    96	 * no later transition ("scan started") to hang it on, since SCANNING is just evidence arriving
    97	 * within the same fixed window, never a new clock start. `undefined` once terminal (never a real
    98	 * candidate again).
    99	 */
   100	export function deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue(file: Pick<DocumentFile, "scanStatus" | "createdAt">): MaintenanceDue | undefined {
   101	  if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return undefined;
   102	  return { dueAtIso: new Date(Date.parse(file.createdAt) + FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS * MS_PER_SECOND).toISOString() };
   103	}

   105	/** `GSI8PK=WORK#DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION` / `GSI8SK=<dueAtIso>#TENANT#<tenantId>#<fileId>`
   106	 * (D-179's exact key spec, same shape as `membershipGsi8Keys()`/`invitationGsi8Keys()`) —
   107	 * `documentId`/`seq` are not embedded here since a `KEYS_ONLY` GSI8 Query already returns the
   108	 * base table's own `PK`/`SK`, which already encode them (`documentFileKey()`). */
   109	export function documentFileGsi8Keys(input: { dueAtIso: string; tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId; fileId: string }): { GSI8PK: string; GSI8SK: string } {
   110	  return {
   111	    GSI8PK: `WORK#${DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION_WORK_TYPE}`,
   112	    GSI8SK: `${input.dueAtIso}#TENANT#${input.tenantId}#${input.fileId}`,
   113	  };
   114	}

   123	export class InvalidFileSetError extends Error {
   124	  constructor(reason: string) {
   125	    super(`Invalid file set for reserveFiles: ${reason}`);
   126	    this.name = "InvalidFileSetError";
   127	  }
   128	}

   130	/** D-143 Decision 6: exactly one PRINCIPAL per batch. Pure validation, checked before any
   131	 * write is attempted — the actual concurrency race between two batches reserving the same
   132	 * DocumentVersion is closed separately, by the transaction's `fileSetSealed` fence in
   133	 * `DocumentArchiveService.reserveFiles()` (D-163 §2), never by this check alone. */
   134	export function assertExactlyOnePrincipal(files: readonly FileUploadSpec[]): void {
   135	  if (files.length === 0) throw new InvalidFileSetError("at least one file is required");
   136	  const principals = files.filter((f) => f.role === "PRINCIPAL");
   137	  if (principals.length !== 1) throw new InvalidFileSetError(`exactly one PRINCIPAL is required, got ${principals.length}`);
   138	}

     1	/**
     2	 * applyFileScanResult / confirmFileScanClean — D-163 §1/§5, first real slice of DocumentFile's
     3	 * scan-result state machine. Reuses `document/domain/document-state-machine.ts`'s
     4	 * `decideNextAction()` VERBATIM (never forked/redesigned — `DocumentFileScanStatus`'s taxonomy
     5	 * is a structural subset of `DocumentStatus`, see `document-file.ts`'s module doc comment).
     6	 *

     8	 * M6's Document (whose `quarantineObject.versionId` is a permanent "" placeholder,

    12	 * `sourceObject` a worker copies from, and does zero writes for that outcome (never claims CLEAN
    13	 * for a copy that hasn't happened, same discipline M6's PROMOTE branch already established).
    14	 * `confirmFileScanClean()` is the confirmation step, called only once that caller's own S3
    15	 * copy+verify has succeeded - mirrors M6's `advanceAfterEvidence()` PROMOTE branch's contract,
    16	 * just as a separate, independently retryable step instead of inlined in the same function.

    19	 * `TenantLifecycleRecord.status = ACTIVE` via `tryTenantBusinessMutation()` — added explicitly to
    20	 * BOTH functions' own transactional writes (never inherited from a caller), same discipline
    21	 * `advance-after-evidence.ts`/`finalizer.ts`/`result-processor.ts` already apply to M6's
    22	 * equivalent writes (W3-07). Before this, these two functions (with zero real call sites) had
    23	 * never been fenced at all — the design's explicit closing item.

    68	const MAX_OCC_RETRIES = 10;
    69	
    70	/** Same get-or-create shape as `DocumentArchiveService.ensureStorageQuota()` (kept as a free
    71	 * function here since this module's functions take `deps`, not a class instance) — storage-
    72	 * quota-scoping (D-2xx). */
    73	async function ensureStorageQuota(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId): Promise<TenantStorageQuota> {
    74	  const key = storageQuotaKey(tenantId);
    75	  const existing = await deps.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
    76	  if (existing) return existing;
    77	  const created = defaultStorageQuota(tenantId, (deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString()))());
    78	  const wrote = await deps.store.putIfAbsent(created);
    79	  if (wrote) return created;
    80	  const fresh = await deps.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
    81	  if (!fresh) throw new Error(`Storage quota record vanished after creation race for tenant ${tenantId}.`);
    82	  return fresh;
    83	}
    84	
    85	/** Re-reads the file fresh on every attempt - never assumes the caller's in-memory copy is
    86	 * current, since the upload-finalizer and malware-scan events for the SAME file may land
    87	 * concurrently, exactly the corridor `decideNextAction()`'s doc comment describes for M6. */
    88	export async function applyFileScanResult(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, input: ApplyFileScanResultInput): Promise<ApplyFileScanResultOutcome> {
    89	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
    90	  const key = documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId);
    91	
    92	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
    93	    const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
    94	    if (!file) return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" };
    95	
    96	    // Terminal-state idempotency FIRST, before any correlation check: a repeated/late event
    97	    // against a file already at CLEAN/REJECTED/UNSUPPORTED/TIMEOUT is a legitimate replay, never
    98	    // re-evaluated against the consolidated triple (D-163 §1's correlation only ever applies
    99	    // while `scanStatus IN (PENDING_UPLOAD, SCANNING)`).
   100	    if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" };
   101	
   102	    // D-163 §1 symmetric evidence correlation: `quarantineObject.versionId === ""` means no
   103	    // physical event has consolidated it yet (`reserveFiles()`'s placeholder) - this event IS
   104	    // the first, so it wins unconditionally. Once consolidated, every further event must match
   105	    // that exact triple or is a stale/duplicate/wrong-object event, never re-consolidated.
   106	    const consolidated = file.quarantineObject.versionId !== "";
   107	    if (consolidated && !sameObjectVersion(file.quarantineObject, input.observedObject)) {
   108	      return { outcome: "IGNORED_WRONG_VERSION" };
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    const mergedUploadEvidence = input.uploadEvidence ?? file.uploadEvidence;
   112	    const mergedMalwareEvidence = input.malwareEvidence ?? file.malwareEvidence;
   113	
   114	    const decision = decideNextAction({
   115	      currentStatus: file.scanStatus,
   116	      uploadValid: mergedUploadEvidence?.valid,
   117	      uploadEvidence: mergedUploadEvidence,
   118	      malwareEvidence: mergedMalwareEvidence,
   119	    });
   120	
   121	    const evidenceSet: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   122	    if (input.uploadEvidence) evidenceSet["uploadEvidence"] = input.uploadEvidence;
   123	    if (input.malwareEvidence) evidenceSet["malwareEvidence"] = input.malwareEvidence;
   124	    const quarantineObjectSet = consolidated ? {} : { quarantineObject: input.observedObject };
   125	
   126	    if (decision.action === "IGNORE_STALE_EVENT") return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" };
   127	
   128	    if (decision.action === "AWAIT_MORE_EVIDENCE") {
   129	      const nowTs = now();
   130	      const result = await tryTenantBusinessMutation({
   131	        store: deps.store,
   132	        tableName: deps.tableName,
   133	        tenantId: input.tenantId,
   134	        entries: [
   135	          {
   136	            Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   137	              tableName: deps.tableName,
   138	              key,
   139	              tenantId: input.tenantId,
   140	              expectedVersion: file.version,
   141	              // SCANNING marks "at least one physical event has landed" independent of
   142	              // decideNextAction's own vocabulary (it only ever returns actions, never this
   143	              // specific status) - a no-op re-SET when the file was already SCANNING.
   144	              set: { scanStatus: "SCANNING", ...quarantineObjectSet, ...evidenceSet },
   145	              now: nowTs,
   146	            }),
   147	          },
   148	        ],
   149	      });
   150	      if (result.ok) return { outcome: "AWAITING" };
   151	      if (result.reason === "OCC_CONFLICT") continue; // concurrent half of evidence landed - retry fresh.
   152	      return { outcome: "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE" };
   153	    }
   154	
   155	    if (decision.action === "PROMOTE") {
   156	      // Never claims CLEAN itself - zero writes here, same discipline M6's PROMOTE branch
   157	      // established (`advance-after-evidence.ts`'s doc comment). The evidence merge that got us
   158	      // here was already persisted by an earlier AWAITING call (PROMOTE is only reachable once
   159	      // BOTH evidences exist, and only one arrives per call) - nothing new to consolidate.
   160	      const sourceObject = consolidated ? file.quarantineObject : input.observedObject;
   161	      return { outcome: "READY_TO_PROMOTE", sourceObject };
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    // decision.action === "REJECT": terminal transition - counters + GSI8 removal happen in the
   165	    // SAME transaction as the file's own write (Decision 6/Bloqueador 9: a crash between the two
   166	    // would leave pendingFileScans stuck above zero forever, permanently blocking acceptVersion).
   167	    const version = await deps.store.get<DocumentVersion>(documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq));
   168	    if (!version) return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" }; // Version cannot be removed once files exist - fail closed if this ever changes.
   169	
   170	    const infected = mergedMalwareEvidence?.status === "THREATS_FOUND";
   171	    const nowTs = now();
   172	    // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): REJECTED/UNSUPPORTED is terminal without ever reaching
   173	    // CLEAN, so the capacity hold `reserveFiles()` took on this file's bytes must be released
   174	    // here — in the SAME transaction as the file's own terminal write, same "counters change
   175	    // atomically with the state transition, never a separate step" discipline this file's
   176	    // pendingFileScans decrement already follows (module doc comment, Decision 6/Bloqueador 9).
   177	    const quota = await ensureStorageQuota(deps, input.tenantId);
   178	    const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   179	      {
   180	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   181	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   182	          key,
   183	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   184	          expectedVersion: file.version,
   185	          set: { scanStatus: decision.status, ...quarantineObjectSet, ...evidenceSet },
   186	          // D-163 §5/§6: any transaction reaching a terminal scanStatus removes the sparse GSI8
   187	          // reconciliation pointer in the same write - never a separate cleanup step.
   188	          remove: ["GSI8PK", "GSI8SK"],
   189	          now: nowTs,
   190	        }),
   191	      },
   192	      {
   193	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   194	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   195	          key: documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq),
   196	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   197	          expectedVersion: version.version,
   198	          set: { pendingFileScans: version.pendingFileScans - 1, ...(infected ? { infectedFileScans: version.infectedFileScans + 1 } : {}) },
   199	          now: nowTs,
   200	        }),
   201	      },
   202	      {
   203	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   204	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   205	          key: storageQuotaKey(input.tenantId),
   206	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   207	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   208	          set: { reservedBytes: Math.max(0, quota.reservedBytes - file.contentLength) },
   209	          now: nowTs,
   210	        }),
   211	      },
   212	    ];
   213	    // D-163 §2 (Rodada 4): FILE_REJECTED_INFECTED is emitted only for the infection-caused
   214	    // REJECTED transition, never for a plain upload-invalid REJECTED or an UNSUPPORTED one - the
   215	    // audit trail exists specifically so "why was this file removed from consideration" is
   216	    // answerable without re-deriving it from malwareEvidence.
   217	    if (infected) {
   218	      entries.push({
   219	        Put: buildVersionedCreate(
   220	          deps.tableName,
   221	          buildFileRejectedEvent(deps, input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, version.versionId, version.state, input.fileId, file.scanStatus, decision.status, nowTs) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey,
   222	        ),
   223	      });
   224	    }
   225	    const result = await tryTenantBusinessMutation({ store: deps.store, tableName: deps.tableName, tenantId: input.tenantId, entries });
   226	    if (result.ok) return { outcome: "REJECTED", status: decision.status };
   227	    if (result.reason === "OCC_CONFLICT") continue;
   228	    return { outcome: "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE" };
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  throw new Error(`applyFileScanResult exhausted retries for file ${input.fileId} under contention.`);
   232	}
   233	
   234	export type ConfirmFileScanCleanOutcome = "CONFIRMED" | "IGNORED_STALE" | "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE";
   235	
   236	export interface ConfirmFileScanCleanInput {
   237	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
   238	  documentId: string;
   239	  seq: number;
   240	  fileId: string;
   241	  cleanObject: DocumentObjectReference;
   242	}
   243	
   244	/**
   245	 * confirmFileScanClean - the PROMOTE confirmation step, called only once the caller (a future
   246	 * S3/GuardDuty event worker, out of scope for this slice) has already copied `sourceObject` to
   247	 * `cleanObject` and verified it (size/checksum, same discipline M6's `advanceAfterEvidence()`
   248	 * requires before it will persist CLEAN). Idempotent against a file that already moved off
   249	 * SCANNING/PENDING_UPLOAD by the time this is called (e.g. concurrently rejected) - never
   250	 * resurrects a terminal file back toward CLEAN.
   251	 */
   252	export async function confirmFileScanClean(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, input: ConfirmFileScanCleanInput): Promise<ConfirmFileScanCleanOutcome> {
   253	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
   254	  const key = documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId);
   255	
   256	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   257	    const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
   258	    if (!file) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   259	    if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   260	
   261	    const version = await deps.store.get<DocumentVersion>(documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq));
   262	    if (!version) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   263	
   264	    const nowTs = now();
   265	    // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): the moment a file durably becomes CLEAN is the real
   266	    // "commit" point for storage — moves `file.contentLength` from `reservedBytes` to
   267	    // `usedBytes` (net zero to the committed total `reserveFiles()` already checked), in the
   268	    // SAME transaction as the CLEAN transition, same discipline as the pendingFileScans entry
   269	    // beside it.
   270	    const quota = await ensureStorageQuota(deps, input.tenantId);
   271	    const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   272	      {
   273	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   274	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   275	          key,
   276	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   277	          expectedVersion: file.version,
   278	          set: { scanStatus: "CLEAN", cleanObject: input.cleanObject },
   279	          remove: ["GSI8PK", "GSI8SK"],
   280	          now: nowTs,
   281	        }),
   282	      },
   283	      {
   284	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   285	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   286	          key: documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq),
   287	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   288	          expectedVersion: version.version,
   289	          set: { pendingFileScans: version.pendingFileScans - 1 },
   290	          now: nowTs,
   291	        }),
   292	      },
   293	      {
   294	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   295	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   296	          key: storageQuotaKey(input.tenantId),
   297	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   298	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   299	          set: { reservedBytes: Math.max(0, quota.reservedBytes - file.contentLength), usedBytes: quota.usedBytes + file.contentLength },
   300	          now: nowTs,
   301	        }),
   302	      },
   303	    ];
   304	    const result = await tryTenantBusinessMutation({ store: deps.store, tableName: deps.tableName, tenantId: input.tenantId, entries });
   305	    if (result.ok) return "CONFIRMED";
   306	    if (result.reason === "OCC_CONFLICT") continue;
   307	    return "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE";
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  throw new Error(`confirmFileScanClean exhausted retries for file ${input.fileId} under contention.`);
   311	}
   312	
   313	export type ApplyFileScanTimeoutOutcome = "TIMED_OUT" | "IGNORED_STALE";
   314	
   315	export interface ApplyFileScanTimeoutInput {
   316	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
   317	  documentId: string;
   318	  seq: number;
   319	  fileId: string;
   320	  /** The exact GSI8 pointer the reconciliation scan observed (D-163 round4 §3) - the
   321	   * transaction below conditions on it verbatim, so a candidate whose deadline changed or
   322	   * that already reached a terminal state between the scan and this write is naturally
   323	   * skipped (ConditionalCheckFailed -> IGNORED_STALE) rather than double-processed. */
   324	  observedGsi8Pointer: { GSI8PK: string; GSI8SK: string };
   325	}
   326	
   327	/**
   328	 * applyFileScanTimeout - the reconciliation worker's terminal transition
   329	 * (PENDING_UPLOAD/SCANNING -> TIMEOUT), symmetric to applyFileScanResult's REJECT branch but
   330	 * reached by deadline rather than by a physical S3/GuardDuty event. Deliberately reuses the
   331	 * exact same counter/GSI8-removal mechanism (buildVersionedUpdate, no new pattern) - the only
   332	 * new ingredient is conditioning on the observed GSI8 pointer itself, which a physical-event
   333	 * caller never needs (it always re-reads the file fresh and checks scanStatus, never a stale
   334	 * discovery pointer).
   335	 */
   336	export async function applyFileScanTimeout(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, input: ApplyFileScanTimeoutInput): Promise<ApplyFileScanTimeoutOutcome> {
   337	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
   338	  const key = documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId);
   339	
   340	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   341	    const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
   342	    if (!file) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   343	    if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   344	
   345	    const version = await deps.store.get<DocumentVersion>(documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq));
   346	    if (!version) return "IGNORED_STALE"; // Version cannot be removed once files exist - fail closed if this ever changes.
   347	
   348	    const nowTs = now();
   349	    // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): TIMEOUT is terminal without ever reaching CLEAN, same
   350	    // reasoning as the REJECT branch above — release the capacity hold this file's bytes took.
   351	    const quota = await ensureStorageQuota(deps, input.tenantId);
   352	    const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   353	      {
   354	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   355	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   356	          key,
   357	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   358	          expectedVersion: file.version,
   359	          set: { scanStatus: "TIMEOUT" },
   360	          remove: ["GSI8PK", "GSI8SK"],
   361	          // Exact-pointer fence (round4-claude-final.md §3): closes the race where the
   362	          // candidate this scan observed already advanced (new deadline, or terminal) by the
   363	          // time this write lands - never conditioned on `file.version` alone, since a
   364	          // concurrent SCANNING->TIMEOUT-eligible re-write could bump version without changing
   365	          // eligibility in a way the scan already accounted for.
   366	          extraConditions: [
   367	            { expression: "#gsi8pk = :gsi8pk", names: { "#gsi8pk": "GSI8PK" }, values: { ":gsi8pk": input.observedGsi8Pointer.GSI8PK } },
   368	            { expression: "#gsi8sk = :gsi8sk", names: { "#gsi8sk": "GSI8SK" }, values: { ":gsi8sk": input.observedGsi8Pointer.GSI8SK } },
   369	          ],
   370	          now: nowTs,
   371	        }),
   372	      },
   373	      {
   374	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   375	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   376	          key: documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq),
   377	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   378	          expectedVersion: version.version,
   379	          set: { pendingFileScans: version.pendingFileScans - 1 },
   380	          now: nowTs,
   381	        }),
   382	      },
   383	      {
   384	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   385	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   386	          key: storageQuotaKey(input.tenantId),
   387	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   388	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   389	          set: { reservedBytes: Math.max(0, quota.reservedBytes - file.contentLength) },
   390	          now: nowTs,
   391	        }),
   392	      },
   393	    ];
   394	    try {
   395	      await deps.store.transactWrite(entries);
   396	      return "TIMED_OUT";
   397	    } catch (err) {
   398	      // A cancellation here may be the GSI8-pointer race the comment above describes, OR a
   399	      // concurrent write to the quota row (e.g. another file's REJECT/CLEAN transition landing
   400	      // at the same instant) - both are legitimately retried by the reconciliation worker's next
   401	      // sweep (it re-derives GSI8 candidates fresh each run), so IGNORED_STALE is correct for
   402	      // either cause, never a silent quota-accounting drop.
   403	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) return "IGNORED_STALE"; // lost the race - a concurrent event/sweep already claimed this file.
   404	      throw err;
   405	    }
   406	  }
   407	
   408	  throw new Error(`applyFileScanTimeout exhausted retries for file ${input.fileId} under contention.`);
   409	}
   410	
   411	function buildFileRejectedEvent(
   412	  deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps,
   413	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId,
   414	  documentId: string,
   415	  seq: number,
   416	  versionId: string,
   417	  versionState: DocumentVersionEvent["toState"],
   418	  fileId: string,
   419	  fromFileScanStatus: DocumentFileScanStatus,
   420	  toFileScanStatus: DocumentFileScanStatus,
   421	  occurredAt: string,
   422	): DocumentVersionEvent {
   423	  return {
   424	    ...documentVersionEventKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, deps.ids.newEventId()),
   425	    entityType: "DocumentVersionEvent",
   426	    tenantId,
   427	    documentId,
   428	    versionId,
   429	    type: "FILE_REJECTED_INFECTED",
   430	    // `fromState`/`toState` record the Version's own (unchanged) state as informational context
   431	    // only - the real transition this event carries is fromFileScanStatus/toFileScanStatus
   432	    // below, per document-version-event.ts's doc comment on this event type.
   433	    fromState: versionState,
   434	    toState: versionState,
   435	    fileId,
   436	    fromFileScanStatus,
   437	    toFileScanStatus,
   438	    actor: "SYSTEM",
   439	    occurredAt,
   440	  };
   441	}

codex
A estrutura central confere até aqui: o roteador já reconhece a chave `document-archive/...`, e a máquina de scan realmente opera por `DocumentFile.scanStatus` sem condicionar o estado da `DocumentVersion`. Estou aprofundando agora os pontos em que a proposta pode divergir do mecanismo existente: quota, idempotência/replay, GSI8 de timeout, presign e wiring da Lambda guest.

/**
 * DocumentService — implementation-blueprint.md §12.1 interface (`reserveUpload`). Quota
 * consumption happens at the HTTP handler layer before calling this service, matching the
 * established pattern in this codebase (item-handlers.ts's `consumeApiRequestQuota` is a
 * separate call, not folded into the same DynamoDB transaction as the entity write) — not a
 * deviation from the blueprint's conceptual "transação" language, just reuse of the same
 * TenantQuotaService every other module already uses instead of inventing a bespoke one.
 */
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { RequestContext } from "../../identity/domain/request-context.js";
import { authorize } from "../../identity/domain/authorization.js";
import { ValidationError, ConflictError, NotFoundError, TenantNotActiveError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
import { buildVersionedCreate } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/occ.js";
import { executeTenantBusinessMutation } from "../../../shared/tenant-lifecycle/tenant-business-mutation.js";
import { documentKey, type Document } from "../domain/document.js";
import { uploadSlotKey, type UploadSlot } from "../domain/upload-slot.js";
import { MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES } from "./upload-validation.js";
import { GSI6PK_RECON_UPLOAD_PENDING, buildUploadSlotGsi6Sk, type DocumentStore, type TransactWriteEntry } from "../ports/document-store.js";
import type { UploadUrlSigner } from "../ports/upload-url-signer.js";
import type { DocumentIdGenerator } from "./id-generator.js";
import { IdempotencyStore, transitionIdempotencyStatus, type DynamoLike } from "../../../shared/idempotency/idempotency.js";

const ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["application/pdf", "image/jpeg", "image/png"]);
const PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS = 600; // 10 minutes, M6 design §2 (fluxo de reserva).
const OPERATION = "document.reserve-upload";

export interface ReserveUploadInput {
  fileName: string;
  mediaType: string;
  contentLength: number;
  checksumSha256: string;
}

export interface ReserveUploadResult {
  documentId: string;
  uploadSlotId: string;
  uploadUrl: string;
  requiredHeaders: Record<string, string>;
  expiresAt: string;
}

export interface DocumentServiceDeps {
  store: DocumentStore;
  tableName: string;
  quarantineBucket: string;
  ids: DocumentIdGenerator;
  signer: UploadUrlSigner;
  now?: () => string;
}

export class DocumentService {
  private readonly store: DocumentStore;
  private readonly tableName: string;
  private readonly quarantineBucket: string;
  private readonly ids: DocumentIdGenerator;
  private readonly signer: UploadUrlSigner;
  private readonly now: () => string;
  private readonly idempotency: IdempotencyStore;

  constructor(deps: DocumentServiceDeps) {
    this.store = deps.store;
    this.tableName = deps.tableName;
    this.quarantineBucket = deps.quarantineBucket;
    this.ids = deps.ids;
    this.signer = deps.signer;
    this.now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
    const adapter: DynamoLike = {
      putIfAbsent: async (item) => ((await this.store.putIfAbsent(item)) ? "PUT" : "ALREADY_EXISTS"),
      get: (key) => this.store.get(key),
      update: (item) => this.store.update(item),
      transitionIfStatus: (item, expectedStatus) => transitionIdempotencyStatus(this.store, this.tableName, item, expectedStatus),
    };
    this.idempotency = new IdempotencyStore(adapter, this.tableName, this.now);
  }

  async reserveUpload(ctx: RequestContext, itemId: string, input: ReserveUploadInput, idempotencyKey: string): Promise<ReserveUploadResult> {
    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "document:reserve-upload", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });

    if (!ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.has(input.mediaType)) {
      throw new ValidationError("Unsupported media type.", { mediaType: input.mediaType });
    }
    if (input.contentLength <= 0 || input.contentLength > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) {
      throw new ValidationError("contentLength must be between 1 and 10MiB.", { contentLength: input.contentLength, maxBytes: MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES });
    }
    if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(input.checksumSha256)) {
      throw new ValidationError("checksumSha256 must be a 64-character hex SHA-256 digest.");
    }

    const requestHash = `${itemId}|${input.fileName}|${input.mediaType}|${input.contentLength}|${input.checksumSha256}`;
    const now = this.now();
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.parse(now) + PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS * 1000).toISOString();

    const begin = await this.idempotency.begin({
      tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId,
      operation: OPERATION,
      key: idempotencyKey,
      requestHash,
      expiresAt,
    });

    let documentId: string;
    let uploadSlotId: string;
    let quarantineKey: string;

    if (begin === "COMPLETED_SAME_REQUEST") {
      // Retry of the exact same request within the idempotency window: reuse the SAME
      // document/slot/key already created by the first attempt (never mint new IDs or a new
      // quarantine key for a request that already succeeded) - same pattern as
      // ExpirationService.renewItem's COMPLETED_SAME_REQUEST branch.
      const record = await this.store.get({
        PK: `TENANT#${ctx.tenant.tenantId}#IDEMPOTENCY#${OPERATION}`,
        SK: `KEY#${idempotencyKey}`,
      });
      const existingDocumentId = (record as { responseRef?: string } | undefined)?.responseRef;
      if (!existingDocumentId) {
        throw new ConflictError("reserveUpload idempotency record missing responseRef.", { itemId });
      }
      const existingDocument = await this.store.get<Document>(documentKey(ctx.tenant.tenantId, itemId, existingDocumentId));
      if (!existingDocument) {
        throw new ConflictError("reserveUpload idempotency record points at a missing Document.", { itemId, documentId: existingDocumentId });
      }
      documentId = existingDocument.documentId;
      uploadSlotId = existingDocument.uploadSlotId;
      quarantineKey = existingDocument.quarantineObject.key;
    } else {
      documentId = this.ids.newDocumentId();
      uploadSlotId = this.ids.newUploadSlotId();
      // Key intentionally encodes tenantId/itemId/documentId/uploadSlotId (M6 design §2 "fluxo
      // de reserva", example key shape) - never the original file name (real PII, excluded).
      // The S3 event that later reaches UploadFinalizerWorker/MalwareResultWorker carries only
/**
 * Pure validation of an observed S3 object against what was declared at reservation time —
 * M6 design §3.2 (`UploadFinalizerWorker` "confirma bucket, key, tamanho, tipo e metadados
 * esperados; rejeita objeto maior que 10 MB").
 */
export const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024; // 10 MiB — capacity-model.md ASSUMPTION closed by M6 design.

export interface DeclaredUpload {
  mediaType: string;
  contentLength: number;
  checksumSha256: string;
}

export interface ObservedUpload {
  mediaType: string;
  contentLength: number;
  checksumSha256?: string;
}

export type UploadValidationResult = "VALID" | "SIZE_MISMATCH" | "SIZE_EXCEEDS_LIMIT" | "MEDIA_TYPE_MISMATCH" | "CHECKSUM_MISMATCH";

export function validateObservedUpload(declared: DeclaredUpload, observed: ObservedUpload): UploadValidationResult {
  if (observed.contentLength > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) return "SIZE_EXCEEDS_LIMIT";
  if (observed.contentLength !== declared.contentLength) return "SIZE_MISMATCH";
  if (observed.mediaType !== declared.mediaType) return "MEDIA_TYPE_MISMATCH";
  if (observed.checksumSha256 !== undefined && observed.checksumSha256 !== declared.checksumSha256) return "CHECKSUM_MISMATCH";
  return "VALID";
}
/** Real presigned-URL adapter for UploadUrlSigner (M6 design §3.1). Presigns a PutObject
 * restricted to the exact key/checksum/content-type/size the caller declared - never grants a
 * broader operation, never grants read. `If-None-Match: *` (not a standard presignable header
 * for PutObject; instead we rely on the opaque random key never colliding, per design) is not
 * used - "no overwrite" is achieved by the key itself being a fresh random value per
 * reservation, matching the blueprint's actual mechanism. */
import { PutObjectCommand, S3Client } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";
import type { PresignUploadInput, PresignUploadResult, UploadUrlSigner } from "../ports/upload-url-signer.js";

export class S3UploadUrlSigner implements UploadUrlSigner {
  constructor(private readonly client: S3Client) {}

  async presignUpload(input: PresignUploadInput): Promise<PresignUploadResult> {
    const command = new PutObjectCommand({
      Bucket: input.bucket,
      Key: input.key,
      ContentType: input.mediaType,
      ContentLength: input.contentLength,
      ChecksumSHA256: Buffer.from(input.checksumSha256, "hex").toString("base64"),
      ServerSideEncryption: "aws:kms",
      Metadata: input.metadata,
    });
    const uploadUrl = await getSignedUrl(this.client, command, { expiresIn: input.expiresInSeconds });
    return {
      uploadUrl,
      requiredHeaders: {
        "Content-Type": input.mediaType,
        "Content-Length": String(input.contentLength),
        "x-amz-checksum-sha256": Buffer.from(input.checksumSha256, "hex").toString("base64"),
        "x-amz-server-side-encryption": "aws:kms",
      },
    };
  }
}

   510	  queue_name               = "${local.name_prefix}-guest-credential-issuance"
/**
 * Handler for /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}* (D-143 Decision 4, D-146) —
 * SEPARATE Lambda from `document-archive-handler.ts` (`authorization_type = NONE` at the API
 * Gateway route, no Cognito JWT authorizer), same isolation posture as `guest-documents-handler.ts`
 * (subject module's M10 precedent). Never uses `APIGatewayProxyEventV2WithJWTAuthorizer`/
 * `extractClaims` — there is no JWT on this route.
 */
import type { APIGatewayProxyEventV2, APIGatewayProxyStructuredResultV2 } from "aws-lambda";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { createDocumentClient } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/client.js";
import { buildDocumentArchiveGuestDeps } from "../composition/document-archive.js";
import {
  handleGetGuestRequest,
  handleStartGuestSession,
  handleSubmitEvidence,
  handleListGuestDocumentTypes,
  type GuestArchiveHttpDeps,
  type GuestArchiveHttpRequest,
} from "../../../modules/document-archive/http/document-archive-guest-handlers.js";
import { toAppError, ValidationError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
import { runWithContext } from "../../../shared/observability/context.js";

const client = createDocumentClient();
const tableName = process.env["TABLE_NAME"];
const guestAccessPepper = process.env["DOCARCHIVE_GUEST_ACCESS_PEPPER"];
if (!tableName) throw new Error("TABLE_NAME env var is required.");
if (!guestAccessPepper) throw new Error("DOCARCHIVE_GUEST_ACCESS_PEPPER env var is required.");
const { guestAccess } = buildDocumentArchiveGuestDeps(client, tableName, guestAccessPepper);
const deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps = { guestAccess };

function parseBody<T>(event: APIGatewayProxyEventV2): T | undefined {
  if (!event.body) return undefined;
  const raw = event.isBase64Encoded ? Buffer.from(event.body, "base64").toString("utf-8") : event.body;
  return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
}

function toApiGatewayResult(response: { statusCode: number; headers: Record<string, string>; cookies?: string[]; body: Record<string, unknown> }): APIGatewayProxyStructuredResultV2 {
  return { statusCode: response.statusCode, headers: response.headers, cookies: response.cookies, body: JSON.stringify(response.body) };
}

export async function handler(event: APIGatewayProxyEventV2): Promise<APIGatewayProxyStructuredResultV2> {
  // correlationId never derives from guest-supplied data (public route) — always generated here.
  return runWithContext({ correlationId: randomUUID() }, () => handleGuestArchiveRoute(event));
}

async function handleGuestArchiveRoute(event: APIGatewayProxyEventV2): Promise<APIGatewayProxyStructuredResultV2> {
  const routeKey = event.routeKey;
  const base: GuestArchiveHttpRequest = {
    pathParameters: event.pathParameters,
    headers: event.headers,
    sourceIp: event.requestContext.http.sourceIp,
  };

  const response = await (async () => {
    try {
      switch (routeKey) {
        case "GET /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}":
          return await handleGetGuestRequest(deps, base);
        case "POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/session":
          return await handleStartGuestSession(deps, base);
        case "POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/uploads":
          return await handleSubmitEvidence(deps, { ...base, body: parseBody(event) });
        case "GET /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/document-types":
          return await handleListGuestDocumentTypes(deps, base);
        default:
          throw new ValidationError(`Unknown route: ${routeKey}`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const appError = toAppError(err);
      return { statusCode: appError.category === "VALIDATION" ? 400 : 500, headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "referrer-policy": "no-referrer" }, body: appError.toJSON() };
    }
  })();

  return toApiGatewayResult(response);
}

     1	/**
     2	 * HTTP handlers for /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}* — D-143 Decision 4.
     3	 * SECOND public (no-JWT) route family in this codebase (after `subject/http/guest-handlers.ts`),

    16	import { AppError, ValidationError, toAppError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
    17	import { defaultSchemaRegistry } from "../../../shared/contracts/schema-validator.js";

    20	const GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_session";
    21	const GUEST_CSRF_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_csrf";
    22	const CSRF_HEADER_NAME = "x-csrf-token";
    23	
    24	interface CookieAttributes {
    25	  httpOnly: boolean;
    26	  maxAgeSeconds: number;
    27	}
    28	
    29	function buildSetCookieHeader(name: string, value: string, attrs: CookieAttributes): string {
    30	  const parts = [`${name}=${value}`, "Path=/", `Max-Age=${attrs.maxAgeSeconds}`, "SameSite=Strict", "Secure"];
    31	  if (attrs.httpOnly) parts.push("HttpOnly");
    32	  return parts.join("; ");
    33	}
    34	
    35	function parseCookieHeader(header: string | undefined): Record<string, string> {
    36	  if (!header) return {};
    37	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    38	  for (const pair of header.split(";")) {
    39	    const eq = pair.indexOf("=");
    40	    if (eq === -1) continue;
    41	    const name = pair.slice(0, eq).trim();
    42	    const value = pair.slice(eq + 1).trim();
    43	    if (name) out[name] = value;
    44	  }
    45	  return out;
    46	}
    47	
    48	function validateAgainstSchema(schemaId: string, body: unknown): void {
    49	  const { valid, errors } = defaultSchemaRegistry.validate(schemaId, body);
    50	  if (!valid) throw new ValidationError("Request body failed schema validation.", { errors });
    51	}

    55	export interface GuestArchiveHttpRequest<TBody = unknown> {
    56	  pathParameters?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
    57	  headers?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
    58	  sourceIp: string;
    59	  body?: TBody;
    60	}

    64	  headers: Record<string, string>;

    66	  body: Record<string, unknown>;
    67	}
