    73	const STATUS_BY_CATEGORY: Record<string, number> = {
    74	  VALIDATION: 400,
    75	  AUTH: 401,
    76	  AUTHORIZATION: 403,
    77	  NOT_FOUND: 404,
    78	  CONFLICT: 409,
    79	  QUOTA_EXCEEDED: 429,
    80	  DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE: 503,
    81	  INTERNAL: 500,
    82	};
    83	
    84	/** Every response from this handler set carries `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` — Decision 4. */
    85	function baseHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
    86	  return { "content-type": "application/json", "referrer-policy": "no-referrer" };
    87	}
    88	
    89	function toResponse(appError: AppError): GuestArchiveHttpResponse {
    90	  const status = STATUS_BY_CATEGORY[appError.category] ?? 500;
    91	  return { statusCode: status, headers: baseHeaders(), body: appError.toJSON() };
    92	}
    93	
    94	async function withErrorMapping(fn: () => Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse>): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
    95	  try {
    96	    return await fn();
    97	  } catch (err) {
    98	    return toResponse(err instanceof AppError ? err : toAppError(err));
    99	  }
   100	}
   101	
   102	function requireToken(req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): string {
   103	  const token = req.pathParameters?.["token"];
   104	  if (!token) throw new ValidationError("Missing token path parameter.");
   105	  return token;
   106	}
   107	
   108	function cookiesOf(req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Record<string, string> {
   109	  return parseCookieHeader(req.headers?.["cookie"] ?? req.headers?.["Cookie"]);
   110	}
   111	
   112	/** GET /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token} — resolves the credential (layer 1)
   113	 * only. Never mints a session (Decision 4). */
   114	export async function handleGetGuestRequest(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
   115	  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
   116	    const token = requireToken(req);
   117	    const resolved = await deps.guestAccess.resolveCredential(token, { ip: req.sourceIp });
   118	    return {
   119	      statusCode: 200,
   120	      headers: baseHeaders(),
   121	      body: {
   122	        request: {
   123	          requirementId: resolved.request.requirementId,
   124	          status: resolved.request.status,
   125	          deadline: resolved.request.deadline,
   126	        },
   127	        // G02 (Block 6, D-2xx) — see ResolvedCredential's own doc comment (guest-document-
   128	        // access-service.ts): best-effort display names, absent when the Subject/Requirement no
   129	        // longer exists.
   130	        subjectDisplayName: resolved.subjectDisplayName,
   131	        requirementName: resolved.requirementName,
   132	      },
   133	    };
   134	  });
   135	}
   136	
   137	/** POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/session — the explicit human
   138	 * interstitial action that mints a GuestSession (layer 2). Sets the HttpOnly session cookie and
   139	 * the readable CSRF cookie. */
   140	export async function handleStartGuestSession(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
   141	  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
   142	    const token = requireToken(req);
   143	    const result = await deps.guestAccess.startGuestSession(token, { ip: req.sourceIp });
   144	    return {
   145	      statusCode: 201,
   146	      headers: baseHeaders(),
   147	      cookies: [
   148	        buildSetCookieHeader(GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, result.session.token, { httpOnly: true, maxAgeSeconds: 30 * 60 }),
   149	        buildSetCookieHeader(GUEST_CSRF_COOKIE_NAME, result.session.csrfToken, { httpOnly: false, maxAgeSeconds: 30 * 60 }),
   150	      ],
   151	      body: { expiresAt: result.expiresAt, subjectDisplayName: result.subjectDisplayName, requirementName: result.requirementName },
   152	    };
   153	  });
   154	}
   155	
   156	/** POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/uploads — layer 3, idempotent
   157	 * evidence submission. Reads the session token from the HttpOnly cookie (never from the request
   158	 * body/path — the guest never re-types it), CSRF from the cookie+header double-submit pair. */
   159	/** GET /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/document-types — discovery route,
   160	 * item 6 of D-173's estado-final-consolidado.md (engineering-only slice, see decisions-log for
   161	 * the full contradiction/pendency writeup). Same anti-enumeration posture as every other guest
   162	 * handler: token validation failures collapse into the identical `GuestAccessInvalidError`
   163	 * response as `handleGetGuestRequest`. */
   164	export async function handleListGuestDocumentTypes(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
   165	  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
   166	    const token = requireToken(req);
   167	    const documentTypes = await deps.guestAccess.listActiveDocumentTypesForGuest(token, { ip: req.sourceIp });
   168	    return {
   169	      statusCode: 200,
   170	      headers: baseHeaders(),
   171	      body: { documentTypes },
   172	    };
   173	  });
   174	}
   175	
   176	export async function handleSubmitEvidence(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest<SubmitEvidenceInput>): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
   177	  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
   178	    requireToken(req); // Presence-checked for route symmetry/observability; resolution is by session cookie, not the path token.
   179	    // Pre-existing gap, found (not introduced) by Codex review round 1 of D-264 (Block 6, G02
   180	    // frontend): the session cookie (GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME) is a single global cookie per
   181	    // browser, never bound to/verified against THIS route's own `token` path segment. Two guest
   182	    // links open in two tabs of the same browser share one cookie jar - the second `session`
   183	    // mint overwrites the first tab's cookie, so the first tab's later `uploads` call resolves
   184	    // against the SECOND session/DocumentRequest, not its own. Real since D-146 (a single-link-
   185	    // at-a-time design); only now reachable via a real multi-tab UI. Fixing this means binding
   186	    // the session to its issuing token (or scoping the cookie name by token) - a session-identity
   187	    // design change that deserves its own scoping, not a rushed patch. Named in decisions-log
   188	    // D-264.
   189	    if (!req.body) throw new ValidationError("Missing request body.");
   190	    validateAgainstSchema(SUBMIT_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA_ID, req.body);
   191	
   192	    const cookies = cookiesOf(req);
   193	    const sessionToken = cookies[GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME];
   194	    // A missing session cookie is itself a guest-auth failure mode (no session to resolve) —
   195	    // collapses into the same generic error as a malformed/expired/wrong-secret token, never a
   196	    // differentiated 400 that would tell a caller "you're just missing a cookie" (an oracle).
   197	    if (!sessionToken) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   198	    const csrfHeaderValue = req.headers?.[CSRF_HEADER_NAME] ?? req.headers?.["X-CSRF-Token"];
   199	
   200	    const result = await deps.guestAccess.submitEvidence(
   201	      sessionToken,
   202	      { ip: req.sourceIp, csrfCookieValue: cookies[GUEST_CSRF_COOKIE_NAME], csrfHeaderValue },
   203	      req.body,
   204	    );
   205	    return { statusCode: 201, headers: baseHeaders(), body: result as unknown as Record<string, unknown> };
   206	  });
   207	}

    95	 * `buildGuestSubmissionDeps` keeps from its authenticated sibling. */

    97	  const store = new DynamoDbDocumentArchiveStore(client, tableName);

   100	  const guestAccess = new GuestDocumentAccessService({ store, tableName, ids, rateLimiter, pepper: guestAccessPepper });
   101	  return { store, guestAccess };
   102	}

     1	/**
     2	 * DocumentFile-flavored UploadFinalizerWorker core logic (D-193 "Ingestão física", slice 1).
     3	 * Sibling to `finalizer.ts`'s `finalizeUpload()` (M6/`Document`) and
     4	 * `submission-finalizer/finalizer.ts`'s `finalizeSubmissionUpload()` (M10/`DocumentSubmission`) —
     5	 * same "head the observed object, validate against what was declared at reservation, hand off to
     6	 * the shared advance-after-evidence step" shape, applied to `document-archive`'s `DocumentFile`.
     7	 */
     8	import { validateObservedUpload } from "../../modules/document/application/upload-validation.js";
     9	import {
    10	  advanceDocumentArchiveFileAfterEvidence,
    11	  type AdvanceDocumentArchiveFileDeps,
    12	} from "../../modules/document-archive/application/advance-file-after-evidence.js";
    13	import { documentFileKey, type DocumentFile } from "../../modules/document-archive/domain/document-file.js";
    14	import { isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled } from "../../modules/extraction/application/document-archive-activation.js";
    15	import type { FeatureFlagsReader } from "../../modules/extraction/ports/feature-flags-reader.js";
    16	import type { AuthorizedTenantId } from "../../modules/identity/domain/authorization.js";
    17	
    18	export interface FinalizeDocumentArchiveUploadInput {
    19	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
    20	  documentId: string;
    21	  seq: number;
    22	  fileId: string;
    23	  object: { bucket: string; key: string; versionId: string };
    24	}
    25	
    26	export type FinalizeDocumentArchiveUploadDeps = AdvanceDocumentArchiveFileDeps & {
    27	  /** D-193 item 8/9 (PROMOTER gate). Checked FIRST, before any DynamoDB/S3 access - while
    28	   * disabled (the default, requires BOTH `EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED` AND
    29	   * `DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED`, see `isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled()`'s own doc
    30	   * comment), this worker is completely inert for `document-archive` uploads - byte-identical
    31	   * to how an unrecognized key shape is handled (logged and dropped upstream in
    32	   * `upload-finalizer-handler.ts`, never retried, never partially applied). */
    33	  featureFlags: FeatureFlagsReader;
    34	};
    35	
    36	export type FinalizeDocumentArchiveOutcome =
    37	  | "CONFIRMED"
    38	  | "REJECTED_INVALID"
    39	  | "IGNORED_UNKNOWN_FILE"
    40	  | "AWAITING"
    41	  | "IGNORED_STALE"
    42	  | "IGNORED_WRONG_VERSION"
    43	  | "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE"
    44	  | "IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED";
    45	
    46	/**
    47	 * Fail-closed at every branch, same posture `finalizeUpload()`'s doc comment establishes for M6:
    48	 * an event whose bucket/key doesn't match this file's own `quarantineObject` is never applied.
    49	 */
    50	export async function finalizeDocumentArchiveUpload(deps: FinalizeDocumentArchiveUploadDeps, input: FinalizeDocumentArchiveUploadInput): Promise<FinalizeDocumentArchiveOutcome> {
    51	  // D-193 item 8/9 (PROMOTER gate) - checked before any store/object access, same fail-closed
    52	  // posture start-ocr.ts's OcrDisabledError already established (a flags-read error is treated
    53	  // identically to the flag being off).
    54	  let flags;
    55	  try {
    56	    flags = await deps.featureFlags.getFlags();
    57	  } catch {
    58	    return "IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED";
    59	  }
    60	  if (!isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled(flags)) return "IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED";
    61	
    62	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
    63	  const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId));
    64	  if (!file) return "IGNORED_UNKNOWN_FILE";
    65	  if (file.quarantineObject.key !== input.object.key || file.quarantineObject.bucket !== input.object.bucket) return "IGNORED_UNKNOWN_FILE";
    66	
    67	  const observed = await deps.objects.headObject(input.object);
    68	  if (!observed) return "REJECTED_INVALID";
    69	
    70	  const validation = validateObservedUpload({ mediaType: file.mediaType, contentLength: file.contentLength, checksumSha256: file.checksumSha256 }, observed);
    71	  const uploadEvidence = {
    72	    object: input.object,
    73	    contentLength: observed.contentLength,
    74	    mediaType: observed.mediaType,
    75	    checksumSha256: observed.checksumSha256 ?? file.checksumSha256,
    76	    valid: validation === "VALID",
    77	    observedAt: now(),
    78	  };
    79	
    80	  const outcome = await advanceDocumentArchiveFileAfterEvidence(deps, {
    81	    tenantId: input.tenantId,
    82	    documentId: input.documentId,
    83	    seq: input.seq,
    84	    fileId: input.fileId,
    85	    observedObject: input.object,
    86	    uploadEvidence,
    87	  });
    88	
    89	  switch (outcome) {
    90	    case "PROMOTED":
    91	      return "CONFIRMED";
    92	    case "REJECTED":
    93	      return "REJECTED_INVALID";
    94	    default:
    95	      return outcome;
    96	  }
    97	}
     1	/**
     2	 * DocumentFile-flavored MalwareResultWorker core logic (D-193 "Ingestão física", slice 1).
     3	 * Sibling to `result-processor.ts`'s `processMalwareResult()` (M6/`Document`) and
     4	 * `submission-malware-result/result-processor.ts`'s `processSubmissionMalwareResult()`
     5	 * (M10/`DocumentSubmission`) — normalizes the GuardDuty finding, dedupes by `scanResultId`,
     6	 * fail-closed on a bucket/key mismatch, then hands off to the shared advance-after-evidence step.
     7	 */
     8	import {
     9	  advanceDocumentArchiveFileAfterEvidence,
    10	  type AdvanceDocumentArchiveFileDeps,
    11	} from "../../modules/document-archive/application/advance-file-after-evidence.js";
    12	import { documentFileKey, type DocumentFile } from "../../modules/document-archive/domain/document-file.js";
    13	import type { GuardDutyScanStatus } from "../../modules/document/domain/malware-scan-result.js";
    14	import { isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled } from "../../modules/extraction/application/document-archive-activation.js";
    15	import type { FeatureFlagsReader } from "../../modules/extraction/ports/feature-flags-reader.js";
    16	import type { AuthorizedTenantId } from "../../modules/identity/domain/authorization.js";
    17	
    18	export interface DocumentArchiveMalwareResultInput {
    19	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
    20	  documentId: string;
    21	  seq: number;
    22	  fileId: string;
    23	  object: { bucket: string; key: string; versionId: string };
    24	  status: GuardDutyScanStatus;
    25	  scanResultId: string;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export type DocumentArchiveMalwareResultDeps = AdvanceDocumentArchiveFileDeps & {
    29	  /** D-193 item 8/9 (PROMOTER gate) - same mechanism/reasoning as
    30	   * `document-archive-finalizer.ts`'s own doc comment on this field. */
    31	  featureFlags: FeatureFlagsReader;
    32	};
    33	
    34	export type DocumentArchiveMalwareResultOutcome =
    35	  | "APPLIED"
    36	  | "IGNORED_UNKNOWN_FILE"
    37	  | "IGNORED_WRONG_OBJECT"
    38	  | "IGNORED_DUPLICATE_SCAN"
    39	  | "AWAITING"
    40	  | "IGNORED_STALE"
    41	  | "IGNORED_WRONG_VERSION"
    42	  | "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE"
    43	  | "IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED";
    44	
    45	export async function processDocumentArchiveMalwareResult(deps: DocumentArchiveMalwareResultDeps, input: DocumentArchiveMalwareResultInput): Promise<DocumentArchiveMalwareResultOutcome> {
    46	  // D-193 item 8/9 (PROMOTER gate) - same fail-closed posture as
    47	  // document-archive-finalizer.ts's finalizeDocumentArchiveUpload().
    48	  let flags;
    49	  try {
    50	    flags = await deps.featureFlags.getFlags();
    51	  } catch {
    52	    return "IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED";
    53	  }
    54	  if (!isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled(flags)) return "IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED";
    55	
    56	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
    57	  const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId));
    58	  if (!file) return "IGNORED_UNKNOWN_FILE";
    59	
    60	  // Fail-closed: never apply a finding to a different bucket/key than the one this file
    61	  // actually reserved (same M6 §3.4 posture `processMalwareResult()` follows).
    62	  if (file.quarantineObject.bucket !== input.object.bucket || file.quarantineObject.key !== input.object.key) {
    63	    return "IGNORED_WRONG_OBJECT";
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  if (file.malwareEvidence?.scanResultId === input.scanResultId) return "IGNORED_DUPLICATE_SCAN";
    67	
    68	  const malwareEvidence = { object: input.object, status: input.status, scanResultId: input.scanResultId, observedAt: now() };
    69	
    70	  const outcome = await advanceDocumentArchiveFileAfterEvidence(deps, {
    71	    tenantId: input.tenantId,
    72	    documentId: input.documentId,
    73	    seq: input.seq,
    74	    fileId: input.fileId,
    75	    observedObject: input.object,
    76	    malwareEvidence,
    77	  });
    78	
    79	  switch (outcome) {
    80	    case "PROMOTED":
    81	    case "REJECTED":
    82	      return "APPLIED";
    83	    default:
    84	      return outcome;
    85	  }
    86	}

   800	   * immutable set of files), closing the race where a caller commits while files are still being
   801	   * added/never reserved at all. The precondition is enforced twice — an in-memory pre-check for
   802	   * a clear `ConflictError` message, and a transactional `ConditionCheck` (TOCTOU-safe, same
   803	   * pattern as the PRINCIPAL fence in `acceptVersion()`, D-163 §5) so a concurrent `reserveFiles()`
   804	   * that seals the set between the read and the write can never be missed. */
   805	  async commitUpload(ctx: RequestContext, documentId: string, seq: number, expectedVersion: number): Promise<DocumentVersion> {
   806	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:upload", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   807	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   808	    const key = documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, seq);
   809	    const current = await this.store.get<DocumentVersion>(key);
   810	    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError("DocumentVersion not found.", { documentId, seq });
   811	    this.assertTransitionOrConflict(current.state, "RECEIVED", documentId, seq);
   812	    if (!current.fileSetSealed) throw new ConflictError("This DocumentVersion's file set is not sealed yet — call reserveFiles() first.", { documentId, seq });
   813	    const now = this.now();
   814	    const gsi5 = reviewQueueGsi5Keys(tenantId, "RECEIVED", now, current.versionId);
   815	    const update = buildVersionedUpdate({
   816	      tableName: this.tableName,
   817	      key,
   818	      tenantId,
   819	      expectedVersion,
   820	      set: { state: "RECEIVED", receivedAt: now, ...gsi5 },
   821	      now,
   822	      extraConditions: [{ expression: "#sealed = :true", names: { "#sealed": "fileSetSealed" }, values: { ":true": true } }],
   823	    });
   824	    try {
   825	      await this.store.transactWrite([{ Update: update }]);
   826	    } catch (err) {
   827	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) throw new ConflictError("DocumentVersion was concurrently modified.", { documentId, seq });
   828	      throw err;
   829	    }
   830	    return { ...current, state: "RECEIVED", receivedAt: now, version: expectedVersion + 1, updatedAt: now };
   831	  }
   832	
   833	  /**
   834	   * G2 (D-247/D-24x) — the tenant-facing review-queue listing route this module was missing:
   835	   * `commitUpload`/`claimReview` above already maintain the sparse AP5/GSI5 index
   836	   * (`reviewQueueGsi5Keys`), removed the instant a Version leaves RECEIVED/UNDER_REVIEW, but
   837	   * nothing ever queried it back out. One state per call (never both merged server-side) —
   838	   * `state` is the GSI5 partition discriminator itself (`reviewQueueGsi5PartitionKey`), the
   839	   * same "one required discriminator per call" shape `searchRequirements`' `status` already
   840	   * uses, not a new pattern. `docarchive:read` (not `docarchive:review`) — this is a read of
   841	   * queue membership, same tier A13's plan entry documents ("Actions: docarchive:read (all)"),
   842	   * distinct from `docarchive:review`'s claim/accept/reject actions on an item once opened. */
   843	  async listReviewQueue(ctx: RequestContext, query: ReviewQueueQuery): Promise<ReviewQueuePage> {
   844	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:read", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   845	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   846	    const page = await this.store.queryIndexPage<DocumentVersion>({
   847	      indexName: "GSI5",
   848	      partitionKeyValue: reviewQueueGsi5PartitionKey(tenantId, query.state),
   849	      limit: SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE,
   850	      exclusiveStartKey: query.exclusiveStartKey,
   851	    });
   852	    const documentIds = [...new Set(page.items.map((v) => v.documentId))];
   853	    const documentRows = documentIds.length > 0 ? await this.store.batchGet<Document>(documentIds.map((documentId) => documentKey(tenantId, documentId))) : [];
   854	    const documentById = new Map(documentRows.map((d) => [d.documentId, d]));
   855	    return {
   856	      items: page.items.map((version) => ({ version, document: documentById.get(version.documentId) })),
   857	      lastEvaluatedKey: page.lastEvaluatedKey,
   858	    };
   859	  }
   860	
   861	  /** RECEIVED -> UNDER_REVIEW. Serializes concurrent reviewers: a second claim attempt fails
   862	   * the OCC condition and receives ConflictError, never silently overwrites the first
   863	   * reviewer's claim (D-143 Decision 1). */
   864	  async claimReview(ctx: RequestContext, documentId: string, seq: number, expectedVersion: number): Promise<DocumentVersion> {
   865	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:review", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   866	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   867	    const reviewerId = ctx.principal.userId;
   868	    const key = documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, seq);
   869	    const current = await this.store.get<DocumentVersion>(key);
   870	    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError("DocumentVersion not found.", { documentId, seq });
   871	    this.assertTransitionOrConflict(current.state, "UNDER_REVIEW", documentId, seq);
   872	    const now = this.now();
   873	    const gsi5 = reviewQueueGsi5Keys(tenantId, "UNDER_REVIEW", now, current.versionId);
   874	    const update = buildVersionedUpdate({
   875	      tableName: this.tableName,
   876	      key,
   877	      tenantId,
   878	      expectedVersion,
   879	      set: { state: "UNDER_REVIEW", reviewerId, ...gsi5 },
   880	      now,
   881	    });
   882	    try {
   883	      await this.store.transactWrite([{ Update: update }]);
   884	    } catch (err) {
   885	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) throw new ConflictError("DocumentVersion is no longer RECEIVED (already claimed or decided).", { documentId, seq });
   886	      throw err;
   887	    }
   888	    return { ...current, state: "UNDER_REVIEW", reviewerId, version: expectedVersion + 1, updatedAt: now };
   889	  }
   890	
   891	  /**
   892	   * acceptVersion — D-143 Decision 2's transaction, Nucleus-1 scope (actions 1-2-3-4-5-6 of
   893	   * the up-to-10 full design; Requirement/DocumentRequest fencing is added when those modules
   894	   * exist). Idempotent: replaying the same `clientRequestToken` with the same effective
   895	   * command returns the persisted `resultSnapshot` rather than re-executing (D-143
   896	   * Decision 2/Bloqueador 4-5) — this increment enforces the idempotency record's uniqueness
   897	   * but does not yet re-derive/compare a `payloadHash` (no varying payload shape to hash yet
   898	   * at Nucleus-1 scope beyond the identifiers already in the transaction's own conditions).
   899	   */
   900	  async acceptVersion(ctx: RequestContext, documentId: string, seq: number, expectedVersion: number, clientRequestToken: string): Promise<AcceptVersionResult> {
   901	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "docarchive:review", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
   902	    const tenantId = authorizedTenantId(ctx);
   903	    const actor = ctx.principal.userId;
   904	
   905	    // Idempotency check FIRST, before any state-transition validation: a legitimate replay of
   906	    // an already-applied `acceptVersion` must return the original result even though the real
   907	    // current state has since moved on (e.g. ACCEPTED -> SUPERSEDED by a later renewal) — D-143
   908	    // Decision 2/Bloqueador 4-5. Checking transition validity first would misclassify a genuine
   909	    // replay as an illegal transition.
   910	    const existingReplay = await this.store.get<IdempotencyRecord<AcceptVersionResult>>(idempotencyRecordKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, clientRequestToken));
   911	    if (existingReplay) return existingReplay.resultSnapshot;
   912	
   913	    const document = await this.getDocumentUnchecked(tenantId, documentId);
   914	    const key = documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, seq);
   915	    const current = await this.store.get<DocumentVersion>(key);
   916	    if (!current) throw new NotFoundError("DocumentVersion not found.", { documentId, seq });
   917	    this.assertReviewerOrAdmin(ctx, current);
   918	    try {
   919	      assertValidDocumentVersionTransition(current.state, "ACCEPTED");
   920	    } catch (err) {
   921	      if (err instanceof InvalidDocumentVersionTransitionError) throw new ConflictError("DocumentVersion is no longer eligible for acceptance.", { documentId, seq, actualState: current.state });
   922	      throw err;
   923	    }
   924	    if (!hasCleanFileScans(current)) {
   925	      throw new ValidationError("DocumentVersion cannot be accepted while file scans are pending or infected.", {
   926	        documentId,
   927	        seq,
   928	        pendingFileScans: current.pendingFileScans,
   929	        infectedFileScans: current.infectedFileScans,
   930	      });
   931	    }
   932	
   933	    const now = this.now();
   934	    const newVersionId = current.versionId;
   935	    const entries = [];
   936	
   937	    // 1. Update Document.currentVersionId
   938	    entries.push({
   939	      Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   940	        tableName: this.tableName,
   941	        key: documentKey(tenantId, documentId),
   942	        tenantId,
   943	        expectedVersion: document.version,
   944	        set: { currentVersionId: newVersionId },
   945	        now,
   946	      }),
   947	    });
   948	
   949	    let previousVersion: DocumentVersion | undefined;
   950	    if (document.currentVersionId && document.currentVersionId !== newVersionId) {
   951	      previousVersion = await this.findVersionById(tenantId, documentId, document.currentVersionId);
   952	      if (previousVersion) {
   953	        // 2. Update previous current Version -> SUPERSEDED
   954	        entries.push({
   955	          Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   956	            tableName: this.tableName,
   957	            key: documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, previousVersion.seq),
   958	            tenantId,
   959	            expectedVersion: previousVersion.version,
   960	            set: { state: "SUPERSEDED" },
   961	            remove: ["GSI5PK", "GSI5SK"],
   962	            now,
   963	            extraConditions: [{ expression: "#docId = :docId AND #st = :acceptedState", names: { "#docId": "documentId", "#st": "state" }, values: { ":docId": documentId, ":acceptedState": "ACCEPTED" } }],
   964	          }),
   965	        });
   966	        // 3. Put DocumentVersionEvent(SUPERSEDED) for the previous version
   967	        entries.push({
   968	          Put: buildVersionedCreate(
   969	            this.tableName,
   970	            this.buildEvent(tenantId, documentId, previousVersion.seq, previousVersion.versionId, "SUPERSEDED", "ACCEPTED", "SUPERSEDED", actor, now),
   971	          ),
   972	        });
   973	      }
   974	    }
   975	
   976	    // 4. Update the new Version -> ACCEPTED
   977	    entries.push({
   978	      Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   979	        tableName: this.tableName,
   980	        key,
   981	        tenantId,
   982	        expectedVersion,
   983	        set: { state: "ACCEPTED", decidedAt: now, reviewerId: actor },
   984	        remove: ["GSI5PK", "GSI5SK"],
   985	        now,
   986	        extraConditions: [
   987	          { expression: "#docId = :docId", names: { "#docId": "documentId" }, values: { ":docId": documentId } },
   988	          { expression: "#st = :received OR #st = :underReview", names: { "#st": "state" }, values: { ":received": "RECEIVED", ":underReview": "UNDER_REVIEW" } },
   989	          { expression: "#pending = :zero", names: { "#pending": "pendingFileScans" }, values: { ":zero": 0 } },
   990	          { expression: "#infected = :zero2", names: { "#infected": "infectedFileScans" }, values: { ":zero2": 0 } },
   991	        ],
   992	      }),
   993	    });
   994	
   995	    // D-163 §5: transactional (not merely in-memory) fence that the PRINCIPAL file is CLEAN —
   996	    // if it was rejected as infected between this read and the transaction, the whole
   997	    // TransactWriteItems cancels (TOCTOU-safe), same as any other ConditionCheck here. Only
   998	    // added when the Version actually has a PRINCIPAL (D-163's `reserveFiles()` is additive —
   999	    // a Version accepted before this slice existed never sets `principalFileId`).
  1000	    if (current.principalFileId) {
  1001	      entries.push(
  1002	        buildExistenceConditionCheck({
  1003	          tableName: this.tableName,
  1004	          key: documentFileKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, current.principalFileId),
  1005	          extra: { scanStatus: "CLEAN" },
  1006	        }),
  1007	      );
  1008	    }
  1009	
  1010	    // 5. Put idempotency record
  1011	    const idempotencyRecord: IdempotencyRecord<AcceptVersionResult> & EntityKey = {
  1012	      ...idempotencyRecordKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, clientRequestToken),
  1013	      entityType: "IdempotencyRecord",
  1014	      tenantId,
  1015	      payloadHash: `acceptVersion:${documentId}:${seq}:${expectedVersion}`,
  1016	      resultSnapshot: { document: { ...document, currentVersionId: newVersionId, version: document.version + 1 }, acceptedVersionId: newVersionId },
  1017	      createdAt: now,
  1018	    };
  1019	    entries.push({ Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, idempotencyRecord as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) });
  1020	
  1021	    // 6. Put DocumentVersionEvent(ACCEPTED) for the new version
  1022	    entries.push({
  1023	      Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, this.buildEvent(tenantId, documentId, seq, newVersionId, "ACCEPTED", current.state, "ACCEPTED", actor, now)),
  1024	    });
  1025	
  1026	    try {
  1027	      await this.store.transactWrite(entries);
  1028	    } catch (err) {
  1029	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) {
  1030	        const replay = await this.store.get<IdempotencyRecord<AcceptVersionResult>>(idempotencyRecordKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, clientRequestToken));
     1	/**
     2	 * D-193 item 8/9 ("Sequenciamento", `estado-final-consolidado.md`) — the mandatory-order
     3	 * activation gate for the `document-archive` OCR/extraction pipeline (D-193 slices 1-7). Two
     4	 * independent AppConfig flags, `EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED` (STARTER) and
     5	 * `DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED` (PROMOTER), activate in a mandatory order — STARTER
     6	 * strictly before PROMOTER, never the reverse — so that a `document-archive` file is never
     7	 * promoted to the CLEAN key while nothing is listening to start extraction against it (the
     8	 * "CLEAN sem consumidor" window the approved design named explicitly).
     9	 *
    10	 * A runbook note alone ("turn on STARTER first, then PROMOTER") only prevents the wrong order
    11	 * when the operator follows it. This function closes the same window BY CONSTRUCTION instead:
    12	 * the promoter path (`upload-finalizer-handler.ts`/`malware-result-handler.ts`'s third branch,
    13	 * reaching `applyFileScanResult`/`confirmFileScanClean`) is gated on THIS function, not on the
    14	 * raw `DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED` value alone — so turning PROMOTION on while STARTER
    15	 * is still off (the forbidden reverse order) can never activate promotion. There is then no code
    16	 * path that produces a CLEAN `document-archive` object while its consumer (the Starter) is off.
    17	 */
    18	import type { FeatureFlags } from "../ports/feature-flags-reader.js";
    19	
    20	/** STARTER alone gates whether `startExtractionRunForDocumentArchive()` may open the
    21	 * ExtractionRun gate / call `startExecution()`. No dependency on PROMOTER — the Starter only
    22	 * ever reacts to an ALREADY-clean object (D-193's own "never trusts the S3 event, always rereads
    23	 * DocumentFile fresh" discipline), so it needs no cross-flag ordering guard of its own. */
    24	export function isDocumentArchiveExtractionTriggerEnabled(flags: FeatureFlags): boolean {
    25	  return flags.EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED === true;
    26	}
    27	
    28	/** PROMOTER requires BOTH flags on, STARTER included — the ordering-safety mechanism itself.
    29	 * While this returns `false` (the default, both flags off), the physical handlers' third branch
    30	 * must treat a `document-archive` quarantine key exactly like an unrecognized key shape: logged
    31	 * and dropped, never retried, never partially applied. */
    32	export function isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled(flags: FeatureFlags): boolean {
    33	  return flags.EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED === true && flags.DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED === true;
    34	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Parses the tenantId/documentId/seq/fileId out of a `document-archive`-namespaced quarantine
     3	 * object key, built verbatim by `DocumentArchiveService.buildQuarantineKey()`:
     4	 * `document-archive/tenant/<tenantId>/document/<documentId>/version/<seq>/file/<fileId>`
     5	 * (D-163 §7).
     6	 *
     7	 * D-193 (`estado-final-consolidado.md`, "Ingestão física") — this is the parser real production
     8	 * `dev` was missing: `upload-finalizer-handler.ts`/`malware-result-handler.ts` only ever tried
     9	 * `document/domain/quarantine-key.ts#parseQuarantineKey` (M6's `tenant/<t>/item/<i>/...` shape)
    10	 * and `subject/domain/submission-quarantine-key.ts#parseSubmissionQuarantineKey` (M10's
    11	 * `tenant/<t>/subject/<s>/...` shape) — a `document-archive/...` key matched NEITHER (this
    12	 * format's leading `document-archive/` segment never appears in either of those patterns), so it
    13	 * fell through to "unrecognized key shape", logged, and silently dropped forever with no retry/
    14	 * DLQ. `DocumentFile`/`DocumentVersion` uploads therefore got stuck in `PENDING_UPLOAD`
    15	 * permanently — this parser plus the third handler branch (see `upload-finalizer-handler.ts`/
    16	 * `malware-result-handler.ts`) is the fix.
    17	 *
    18	 * Never collides with either existing format (`document-archive/` is a segment neither of the
    19	 * other two patterns can ever produce at that position) — same "additive, never overlapping"
    20	 * discipline `submission-quarantine-key.ts`'s own doc comment establishes for its own addition.
    21	 */
    22	export interface ParsedDocumentArchiveQuarantineKey {
    23	  tenantId: string;
    24	  documentId: string;
    25	  seq: number;
    26	  fileId: string;
    27	}
    28	
    29	const KEY_PATTERN = /^document-archive\/tenant\/([^/]+)\/document\/([^/]+)\/version\/([^/]+)\/file\/([^/]+)$/;
    30	
    31	export function parseDocumentArchiveQuarantineKey(key: string): ParsedDocumentArchiveQuarantineKey | undefined {
    32	  const match = KEY_PATTERN.exec(key);
    33	  if (!match) return undefined;
    34	  const [, tenantId, documentId, seqStr, fileId] = match;
    35	  if (!tenantId || !documentId || !seqStr || !fileId) return undefined;
    36	  const seq = Number(seqStr);
    37	  if (!Number.isInteger(seq) || seq <= 0) return undefined;
    38	  return { tenantId, documentId, seq, fileId };
    39	}
     1	/**
     2	 * DocumentVersion — D-143 Decision 1 (state machine) + Decision 6 (file lifecycle).
     3	 * `DocumentVersion.state` is the SOLE mutable source of truth (Decision 3) — the append-only
     4	 * `DocumentVersionEvent` log (`document-version-event.ts`) records every transition but never
     5	 * competes with this field for authority.
     6	 *
     7	 * Graph (estado-final-consolidado.md Decision 1):
     8	 *   (none)      -[reserveUpload]->        DRAFT
     9	 *   DRAFT       -[commitUpload]->         RECEIVED
    10	 *   DRAFT       -[abandonUpload]->        WITHDRAWN
    11	 *   RECEIVED    -[claimReview]->          UNDER_REVIEW
    12	 *   RECEIVED | UNDER_REVIEW -[acceptVersion]-> ACCEPTED
    13	 *   RECEIVED | UNDER_REVIEW -[rejectVersion]-> REJECTED
    14	 *   ACCEPTED    -[superseded by another version's acceptVersion]-> SUPERSEDED
    15	 *   UNDER_REVIEW -[claim TTL sweeper]->    RECEIVED
    16	 *
    17	 * REJECTED is a terminal state that is NEVER removable (D-143 Decision 7 — a Rodada 1 proposal
    18	 * that allowed removing REJECTED versions was rejected for directly contradicting J9's
    19	 * "the rejected file stays in history" requirement). Only DRAFT can ever be physically removed
    20	 * (via WITHDRAWN, before it ever became evidence).
    21	 */
    22	import type { EntityKey } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/occ.js";
    23	import type { AuthorizedTenantId } from "../../identity/domain/authorization.js";
    24	import type { UnifiedValidityState } from "../../../shared/domain/validity-state.js";
    25	import { deriveValidityStateFromExpiry } from "../../../shared/domain/validity-state.js";
    26	
    27	export type DocumentVersionState = "DRAFT" | "RECEIVED" | "UNDER_REVIEW" | "ACCEPTED" | "REJECTED" | "SUPERSEDED" | "WITHDRAWN";
    28	
    29	/** Closed taxonomy, `document-domain-functional-specification-v0.1.md` §12. */
    30	export type RejectionReason = "EXPIRED" | "ILLEGIBLE" | "INCORRECT" | "WRONG_SUBJECT" | "OUTDATED_VERSION" | "INCOMPLETE" | "OTHER";
    31	
    32	/** Origin taxonomy, spec §13. */
    33	export type DocumentVersionOrigin = "MANUAL_UPLOAD" | "GUEST_UPLOAD" | "REQUEST_RESPONSE" | "IMPORT" | "AUTOMATED_CAPTURE";
    34	
    35	export interface DocumentVersion extends EntityKey {
    36	  entityType: "DocumentVersion";
    37	  versionId: string;
    38	  documentId: string;
    39	  tenantId: string;
    40	  seq: number;
    41	  state: DocumentVersionState;
    42	  origin: DocumentVersionOrigin;
    43	  issuedAt?: string;
    44	  validFrom?: string;
    45	  validUntil?: string;
    46	  receivedAt?: string;
    47	  reviewerId?: string;
    48	  decidedAt?: string;
    49	  rejectionReason?: RejectionReason;
    50	  /** Decision 6/Bloqueador 9: two independent counters gate `acceptVersion` — `pendingFileScans=0`
    51	   * alone is NOT sufficient (a version with zero pending and one INFECTED file must never be
    52	   * accepted), both must be zero. */
    53	  pendingFileScans: number;
    54	  infectedFileScans: number;
    55	  /** D-163 §2: set atomically by `reserveFiles()`'s single `TransactWriteItems`, together with
    56	   * `principalFileId`/`totalFiles`/`pendingFileScans` — never by a separate write. Fences two
    57	   * concurrent `reserveFiles()` calls against the same Version: the second one's Update
    58	   * condition (`fileSetSealed` absent or `false`) fails, closing the race a Rodada 1 proposal
    59	   * tried to close with input validation alone (D-163 §2, achado real da Rodada 1). Once
    60	   * `true`, no further file reservation against this Version is possible — the only recovery
    61	   * for a bad file set is rejecting the whole Version and starting a new one (D-143
    62	   * Decision 1/7, never reopening a sealed set). */
    63	  fileSetSealed?: boolean;
    64	  principalFileId?: string;
    65	  totalFiles?: number;
    66	  requestId?: string;
    67	  createdAt: string;
    68	  updatedAt: string;
    69	  version: number;
    70	  /** Present only while state IN (RECEIVED, UNDER_REVIEW) — sparse GSI5 review-queue entry
    71	   * (AP5). Removed (not merely blanked) the moment the version leaves either state, so the
    72	   * index only ever contains live review work (AWS DynamoDB sparse-index pattern). */
    73	  GSI5PK?: string;
    74	  GSI5SK?: string;
    75	}

    77	const SEQ_WIDTH = 6;
    78	
    79	export function formatVersionSeq(seq: number): string {
    80	  return String(seq).padStart(SEQ_WIDTH, "0");
    81	}
    82	
    83	export function documentVersionKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, documentId: string, seq: number): { PK: string; SK: string } {
    84	  return { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#DOCUMENT#${documentId}`, SK: `VERSION#${formatVersionSeq(seq)}` };
    85	}
    86	
    87	/** AP5 sparse review-queue index — separate buckets per real state (never a fixed `RECEIVED`
    88	 * literal for both RECEIVED and UNDER_REVIEW, the exact bug the Rodada 2 proposal had).
    89	 * `reviewQueueGsi5PartitionKey` is split out (same precedent as `requirementGsi9PartitionKey`
    90	 * in `requirement.ts`) so the G2 listing route (`listReviewQueue`, D-24x) can build the exact
    91	 * same partition key a caller queries by, without duplicating the literal template. */
    92	export function reviewQueueGsi5PartitionKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, state: "RECEIVED" | "UNDER_REVIEW"): string {
    93	  return `TENANT#${tenantId}#REVIEWQUEUE#${state}`;
    94	}
    95	
    96	export function reviewQueueGsi5Keys(
    97	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId,
    98	  state: "RECEIVED" | "UNDER_REVIEW",
    99	  orderingTimestamp: string,
   100	  versionId: string,
   101	): { GSI5PK: string; GSI5SK: string } {
   102	  return {
   103	    GSI5PK: reviewQueueGsi5PartitionKey(tenantId, state),
   104	    GSI5SK: `${orderingTimestamp}#VERSION#${versionId}`,
   105	  };
   106	}
   107	
   108	/** AP11 — Version lookup by id alone (Decision 5's relink needs to resolve a `versionId` to
   109	 * its owning `documentId` without knowing the Document upfront). */
   110	export function versionLookupGsi5Keys(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, versionId: string): { GSI5PK: string; GSI5SK: string } {
   111	  return { GSI5PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#VERSIONLOOKUP`, GSI5SK: `VERSION#${versionId}` };
   112	}
   113	
   114	interface TransitionRule {
   115	  from: readonly DocumentVersionState[];
   116	  to: DocumentVersionState;
   117	}
   118	
   119	const TRANSITIONS: readonly TransitionRule[] = [
   120	  { from: ["DRAFT"], to: "RECEIVED" },
   121	  { from: ["DRAFT"], to: "WITHDRAWN" },
   122	  { from: ["RECEIVED"], to: "UNDER_REVIEW" },
   123	  { from: ["UNDER_REVIEW"], to: "RECEIVED" }, // claim TTL sweeper releasing a dead claim
   124	  { from: ["RECEIVED", "UNDER_REVIEW"], to: "ACCEPTED" },
   125	  { from: ["RECEIVED", "UNDER_REVIEW"], to: "REJECTED" },
   126	  { from: ["ACCEPTED"], to: "SUPERSEDED" },
   127	];
   128	
   129	export class InvalidDocumentVersionTransitionError extends Error {
   130	  constructor(
   131	    readonly from: DocumentVersionState,
   132	    readonly to: DocumentVersionState,
   133	  ) {
   134	    super(`Cannot transition DocumentVersion from "${from}" to "${to}"`);
   135	    this.name = "InvalidDocumentVersionTransitionError";
   136	  }
   137	}
   138	
   139	export function canTransitionDocumentVersion(from: DocumentVersionState, to: DocumentVersionState): boolean {
   140	  return TRANSITIONS.some((rule) => rule.to === to && rule.from.includes(from));
   141	}
   142	
   143	/** Throws rather than returning a boolean (implementation-blueprint.md's `authorize()`
   144	 * precedent: callers should not be able to forget to check a boolean result). */
   145	export function assertValidDocumentVersionTransition(from: DocumentVersionState, to: DocumentVersionState): void {
   146	  if (!canTransitionDocumentVersion(from, to)) {
   147	    throw new InvalidDocumentVersionTransitionError(from, to);
   148	  }
   149	}
   150	
   151	/** Decision 7: REJECTED is never removable (would contradict J9's "the rejected file stays in
   152	 * history"). Only DRAFT can ever be removed — WITHDRAWN is the terminal state that records a
   153	 * DRAFT was abandoned before it ever became evidence, and only a version that reached
   154	 * WITHDRAWN this way is eligible for physical deletion under retention policy. */
   155	export function isRemovableDocumentVersionState(state: DocumentVersionState): boolean {
   156	  return state === "DRAFT";
   157	}
   158	
   159	/** Decision 1's terminal states — no further state transition is possible once reached (a
   160	 * repeated command against one of these is only ever a legitimate idempotent replay, never a
   161	 * fresh mutation). */
   162	export function isTerminalDocumentVersionState(state: DocumentVersionState): boolean {
   163	  return state === "ACCEPTED" || state === "REJECTED" || state === "SUPERSEDED" || state === "WITHDRAWN";
   164	}
   165	
   166	/** Decision 6/Bloqueador 9 gate for `acceptVersion` — both counters must be zero. */
   167	export function hasCleanFileScans(version: Pick<DocumentVersion, "pendingFileScans" | "infectedFileScans">): boolean {
   168	  return version.pendingFileScans === 0 && version.infectedFileScans === 0;
   169	}
   170	
   171	/**
   172	 * D-194 fatia 1: `UnifiedValidityState` adapter. `DRAFT` is excluded (`undefined`) — it is not a
   173	 * pending review, just an in-progress upload. `RECEIVED`/`UNDER_REVIEW` -> `AGUARDANDO_REVISAO`.
   174	 * `REJECTED`/`WITHDRAWN`/`SUPERSEDED` are terminal-but-not-current -> excluded. `ACCEPTED`
   175	 * delegates to `deriveValidityStateFromExpiry` (PERMANENTE/VALIDO/VENCENDO/VENCIDO, the only
   176	 * state here that can genuinely be expired). Not consumed by any search mode in this phase (no
   177	 * mode searches `Document`/`DocumentVersion` directly) — exists for a future detail-view use per
   178	 * the design doc.
   179	 */
   180	export function deriveDocumentVersionValidityState(version: Pick<DocumentVersion, "state" | "validUntil">, now: Date): UnifiedValidityState | undefined {
   181	  switch (version.state) {
   182	    case "RECEIVED":
   183	    case "UNDER_REVIEW":
   184	      return "AGUARDANDO_REVISAO";
   185	    case "ACCEPTED":
   186	      return deriveValidityStateFromExpiry(version.validUntil, now);
   187	    default:
   188	      return undefined;
   189	  }
   190	}
     1	/**
     2	 * Narrow port for the DocumentFileReconciliationWorker (D-163/D-166, migrated to GSI8 by D-179
     3	 * slice 3 — 3rd of 9 workers, mirroring `membership-purge`/`invitation-purge`'s own migrations).
     4	 *
     5	 * Replaces the base-table `Scan` filtered by `attribute_exists(GSI5PK)` this worker used through
     6	 * D-166 with a `Query` against GSI8 (`GSI8PK=WORK#DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION`,
     7	 * `GSI8SK=<dueAtIso>#TENANT#<tenantId>#<fileId>`, `KEYS_ONLY`). That Scan was discovered, on
     8	 * re-reading the write path before this migration, to have never had a real writer
     9	 * (`reserveFiles()` never called `fileReconciliationGsi5Keys()`) — this worker could never find a
    10	 * real candidate in production. GSI8 is a clean replacement, not a second mechanism.
    11	 *
    12	 * Unlike `membership-purge`/`invitation-purge`, this worker has no tenant-ACTIVE fence and no
    13	 * poison-record/backoff/DLQ mechanism (D-166's own file header: "a stuck upload is not a
    14	 * retention decision, it never depended on tenant ACTIVE status") — every non-terminal
    15	 * `DocumentFile` is either promoted/rejected by a real physical event or eventually times out,
    16	 * unconditionally, once its deadline passes. GSI8 is discovery-only here too, but the actual
    17	 * claim/revalidation already lives inside `applyFileScanTimeout()` (its own fresh `store.get()` +
    18	 * OCC retry loop + exact-pointer `ConditionCheck`) — this port therefore only needs `queryDue()`,
    19	 * never a separate `getFile()`/`transactWrite()` pair: composing with that existing transactional
    20	 * fence, not duplicating it, is the whole point of this slice.
    21	 *
    22	 * GSI8 has no per-status partition (unlike the old per-status GSI5 namespace), so the two
    23	 * independent bounded scans D-166 ran (one per `PENDING_UPLOAD`/`SCANNING`) collapse into a
    24	 * single due-ordered `Query` covering both statuses at once — a real simplification GSI8 enables,
    25	 * not just a mechanical swap.
    26	 */
    27	import type { EntityKey } from "../../shared/dynamodb/occ.js";
    28	
    29	export interface DocumentFileGsi8Candidate extends EntityKey {
    30	  dueAtIso: string;
    31	  tenantId: string;
    32	  documentId: string;
    33	  seq: number;
    34	  fileId: string;
    35	}
    36	
    37	export interface DocumentFileGsi8Page {
    38	  items: DocumentFileGsi8Candidate[];
    39	  lastEvaluatedKey?: Record<string, unknown>;
    40	}
    41	
    42	export interface DocumentFileReconciliationCandidateSource {
    43	  /** `Query GSI8PK = "WORK#DOCUMENT_FILE_RECONCILIATION" AND GSI8SK < :before`, ordered by due
    44	   * date. `documentId`/`seq`/`fileId` are parsed from the base table's own `PK`/`SK`
    45	   * (`documentFileKey()`'s shape), never re-derived from `GSI8SK` — `KEYS_ONLY` already returns
    46	   * them for free. */
    47	  queryDue(input: { before: string; exclusiveStartKey?: Record<string, unknown> }): Promise<DocumentFileGsi8Page>;
    48	}
     1	/** DocumentFileReconciliationWorker core logic — D-163 §6, migrated off the base-table Scan onto
     2	 * GSI8 by D-179 slice 3 (3rd of 9 workers, `candidate-source.ts`'s doc comment has the full
     3	 * rationale, including why this collapses to a single Query instead of the two independent
     4	 * per-status scans D-166 ran). The actual terminal transition (and the race-closing exact
     5	 * GSI8PK/GSI8SK condition) lives in `apply-file-scan-result.ts`'s `applyFileScanTimeout` — this
     6	 * module only discovers candidates and calls it, unchanged division of labor from D-166. */
     7	import { applyFileScanTimeout, type ApplyFileScanResultDeps } from "../../modules/document-archive/application/apply-file-scan-result.js";
     8	import { documentFileGsi8Keys } from "../../modules/document-archive/domain/document-file.js";
     9	import { authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity } from "../../modules/identity/domain/authorization.js";
    10	import type { DocumentFileGsi8Candidate, DocumentFileReconciliationCandidateSource } from "./candidate-source.js";
    11	
    12	/** Hard cap on pages drained per invocation - same rationale as the purge workers' `MAX_PAGES`:
    13	 * bounds a single invocation against a pathological backlog, the rest picked up by the next
    14	 * scheduled run. */
    15	const MAX_PAGES = 25;
    16	
    17	export interface DocumentFileReconciliationDeps extends ApplyFileScanResultDeps {
    18	  candidates: DocumentFileReconciliationCandidateSource;
    19	  now: () => string;
    20	}
    21	
    22	export interface DocumentFileReconciliationResult {
    23	  scanned: number;
    24	  timedOut: number;
    25	  skippedNotDue: number;
    26	  skippedStale: number;
    27	  /** Age in seconds of the oldest due candidate this run's GSI8 query returned. `undefined` when
    28	   * no candidate was returned at all - same observability shape membership-purge/invitation-purge
    29	   * added for their own GSI8 migrations. */
    30	  oldestCandidateAgeSeconds: number | undefined;
    31	}
    32	
    33	export async function reconcileTimedOutDocumentFiles(deps: DocumentFileReconciliationDeps): Promise<DocumentFileReconciliationResult> {
    34	  const result: DocumentFileReconciliationResult = { scanned: 0, timedOut: 0, skippedNotDue: 0, skippedStale: 0, oldestCandidateAgeSeconds: undefined };
    35	  const nowIso = deps.now();
    36	  const nowMs = Date.parse(nowIso);
    37	
    38	  let exclusiveStartKey: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    39	  for (let page = 0; page < MAX_PAGES; page++) {
    40	    const gsi8Page = await deps.candidates.queryDue({ before: nowIso, exclusiveStartKey });
    41	
    42	    if (page === 0 && gsi8Page.items.length > 0) {
    43	      const oldest = gsi8Page.items[0]!;
    44	      result.oldestCandidateAgeSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((nowMs - Date.parse(oldest.dueAtIso)) / 1000));
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    for (const candidate of gsi8Page.items) {
    48	      await processOneCandidate(deps, candidate, nowMs, result);
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    if (!gsi8Page.lastEvaluatedKey) break;
    52	    exclusiveStartKey = gsi8Page.lastEvaluatedKey;
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  return result;
    56	}
    57	
    58	async function processOneCandidate(
    59	  deps: DocumentFileReconciliationDeps,
    60	  candidate: DocumentFileGsi8Candidate,
    61	  nowMs: number,
    62	  result: DocumentFileReconciliationResult,
    63	): Promise<void> {
    64	  result.scanned += 1;
    65	
    66	  if (Date.parse(candidate.dueAtIso) > nowMs) {
    67	    // Defensive only - queryDue()'s own `GSI8SK < :before` filter means this should never be
    68	    // reachable in practice, but eligibility is always re-derived here, never assumed (same
    69	    // posture as invitation-purge/membership-purge's own defensive check).
    70	    result.skippedNotDue += 1;
    71	    return;
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  const tenantId = authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(candidate);
    75	  const observedGsi8Pointer = documentFileGsi8Keys({ dueAtIso: candidate.dueAtIso, tenantId, fileId: candidate.fileId });
    76	  const outcome = await applyFileScanTimeout(deps, {
    77	    tenantId,
    78	    documentId: candidate.documentId,
    79	    seq: candidate.seq,
    80	    fileId: candidate.fileId,
    81	    observedGsi8Pointer,
    82	  });
    83	
    84	  if (outcome === "TIMED_OUT") result.timedOut += 1;
    85	  else result.skippedStale += 1; // already advanced concurrently (event, or a previous/parallel sweep) - never double-processed.
    86	}

     1	/**
     2	 * G02 (Block 6, D-2xx) — Solicitação de documento (convidado). Deliberately NOT `apiClient`
     3	 * (`api/client.ts`): that client always prefixes `/bff/api` and sends the BFF's OWN CSRF cookie

     6	 * RequestContextResolver/authorize()") with its OWN double-submit CSRF pair

    18	export class GuestUnavailableError extends Error {
    19	  constructor() {
    20	    super("Guest access unavailable.");
    21	    this.name = "GuestUnavailableError";
    22	  }
    23	}
    24	
    25	const CSRF_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_csrf";
    26	
    27	function readCookie(name: string): string | undefined {
    28	  try {
    29	    const match = document.cookie.split("; ").find((row) => row.startsWith(`${name}=`));
    30	    return match?.split("=")[1];
    31	  } catch {
    32	    return undefined;
    33	  }
    34	}
    35	
    36	async function request<T>(path: string, options: { method?: string; body?: unknown; csrf?: boolean } = {}): Promise<T> {
    37	  const method = options.method ?? "GET";
    38	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    39	  if (options.body !== undefined) headers["content-type"] = "application/json";
    40	  if (options.csrf) {
    41	    const token = readCookie(CSRF_COOKIE_NAME);
    42	    if (token) headers["x-csrf-token"] = token;
    43	  }
    44	
    45	  let response: Response;
    46	  try {
    47	    response = await fetch(path, {
    48	      method,
    49	      headers,
    50	      credentials: "include",
    51	      body: options.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined,
    52	    });
    53	  } catch {
    54	    throw new GuestUnavailableError();
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  if (!response.ok) throw new GuestUnavailableError();
    58	
    59	  const text = await response.text();
    60	  return (text ? JSON.parse(text) : undefined) as T;
    61	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Idempotency-key lifecycle (mission §33-35): one logical user submission = one key. A retry
     3	 * of the SAME submission (the mutation function throwing, user clicks "try again" without
     4	 * changing anything) reuses the SAME key - only calling `newIntent()` (after a genuine
     5	 * success, or when the user deliberately starts a new/different submission) generates a
     6	 * fresh one. Never regenerate on every call, which would silently defeat the backend's
     7	 * idempotency protection (src/modules/expiration/application/expiration-service.ts's
     8	 * createItem) - mission §36 is explicit: "Não usar random key em cada retry".
     9	 */
    10	import { useMutation, type UseMutationOptions, type UseMutationResult } from "@tanstack/react-query";
    11	import { useRef } from "react";
    12	
    13	export interface IdempotentMutationOptions<TData, TVariables> extends Omit<UseMutationOptions<TData, unknown, TVariables>, "mutationFn"> {
    14	  mutationFn: (variables: TVariables, idempotencyKey: string) => Promise<TData>;
    15	  /** When provided, the key survives a full-page reload (e.g. the BFF login redirect during a
    16	   * session interruption mid-submission, mission §49) by persisting it to sessionStorage under
    17	   * this key - never localStorage, this key is submission-scoped, not meant to outlive the tab.
    18	   * Read/write failures (private browsing, disabled storage) degrade to a purely in-memory key
    19	   * rather than throwing - the same "unavailable browser API degrades gracefully" convention as
    20	   * api/client.ts's readCookie. Omitting this option preserves the exact prior in-memory-only
    21	   * behavior. */
    22	  persistenceKey?: string;
    23	}
    24	
    25	export type IdempotentMutationResult<TData, TVariables> = UseMutationResult<TData, unknown, TVariables> & {
    26	  /** Call after a successful submission is fully acknowledged (e.g. the user navigates away
    27	   * or explicitly starts filling a new form) - never call this just to "clear" a failed
    28	   * attempt the user might still retry as the SAME intent. */
    29	  newIntent: () => void;
    30	};
    31	
    32	function readPersistedKey(storageKey: string): string | undefined {
    33	  try {
    34	    return window.sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey) ?? undefined;
    35	  } catch {
    36	    return undefined;
    37	  }
    38	}
    39	
    40	function writePersistedKey(storageKey: string, value: string): void {
    41	  try {
    42	    window.sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, value);
    43	  } catch {
    44	    // Storage unavailable - the key still works, it just won't survive a reload.
    45	  }
    46	}
    47	
    48	export function useIdempotentMutation<TData, TVariables>(options: IdempotentMutationOptions<TData, TVariables>): IdempotentMutationResult<TData, TVariables> {
    49	  const keyRef = useRef<string>();
    50	  if (keyRef.current === undefined) {
    51	    const persisted = options.persistenceKey ? readPersistedKey(options.persistenceKey) : undefined;
    52	    keyRef.current = persisted ?? crypto.randomUUID();
    53	    if (options.persistenceKey && !persisted) {
    54	      writePersistedKey(options.persistenceKey, keyRef.current);
    55	    }
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  const mutation = useMutation<TData, unknown, TVariables>({
    59	    ...options,
    60	    mutationFn: (variables: TVariables) => options.mutationFn(variables, keyRef.current as string),
    61	  });
    62	
    63	  return {
    64	    ...mutation,
    65	    newIntent: () => {
    66	      const fresh = crypto.randomUUID();
    67	      keyRef.current = fresh;
    68	      if (options.persistenceKey) writePersistedKey(options.persistenceKey, fresh);
    69	    },
    70	  };
    71	}

   239	function StepFile({
   240	  headingRef,
   241	  file,
   242	  fileError,
   243	  onFileSelected,
   244	  onBack,
   245	  onContinue,
   246	}: {
   247	  headingRef: React.RefObject<HTMLHeadingElement>;
   248	  file: File | undefined;

   255	    event.preventDefault();

   263	  }

   273	          Selecionar arquivo
   274	          <input type="file" accept={ACCEPTED_TYPES.join(",")} onChange={handleChange} className="u-visually-hidden" />
   275	        </label>
   276	        <p>PDF, JPG ou PNG · até 10 MB</p>
   277	      </div>
   278	      {fileError ? (
   279	        <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert">
   280	          {fileError}
   281	        </InlineNotice>
   282	      ) : file ? (
   283	        <p>Selecionado: {file.name}</p>
   284	      ) : null}
   285	      <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onBack}>
   286	        Voltar
   287	      </Button>{" "}
   288	      <Button variant="primary" disabled={!file} onClick={onContinue}>
   289	        Continuar
   290	      </Button>
   291	    </div>
   292	  );
   293	}

   295	function StepReview({
   296	  headingRef,
   297	  token,
   298	  documentTypeId,
   299	  file,
   300	  onBack,
   301	  onSubmitted,
   302	}: {
   303	  headingRef: React.RefObject<HTMLHeadingElement>;
   304	  token: string;
   305	  documentTypeId: string;
   306	  file: File;
   307	  onBack: () => void;
   308	  onSubmitted: () => void;
   309	}) {
   310	  const typesQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: ["guest", "documentTypes", token], queryFn: () => listGuestDocumentTypes(token), retry: false });
   311	  const typeLabel = typesQuery.data?.documentTypes.find((t: GuestDocumentTypeOption) => t.documentTypeId === documentTypeId)?.displayName ?? documentTypeId;
   312	
   313	  const mutation = useIdempotentMutation<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof submitGuestEvidence>>, void>({
   314	    mutationFn: (_input, idempotencyKey) => submitGuestEvidence(token, { fileName: file.name, documentTypeId, idempotencyKey }),
   315	  });
   316	
   317	  async function handleSubmit() {
   318	    try {
   319	      await mutation.mutateAsync(undefined);
   320	      onSubmitted();
   321	    } catch {
   322	      // Error surfaced via mutation.isError below - generic copy, see this file's header
   323	      // comment (deviation 2: the backend never distinguishes submission failure causes).
   324	    }
   325	  }
   326	
   327	  return (
   328	    <div>
   329	      <h1 ref={headingRef} tabIndex={-1}>
   330	        Revisar e enviar
   331	      </h1>
   332	      <p>
   333	        <strong>Tipo de documento:</strong> {typeLabel}
   334	      </p>
   335	      <p>
   336	        <strong>Arquivo:</strong> {file.name}
   337	      </p>
   338	      <InlineNotice tone="neutral">
   339	        Revise antes de enviar. Após o envio, uma pessoa da equipe responsável pode entrar em contato pelos mesmos meios usados para enviar este link, pedindo a correção de um
   340	        campo específico — mas você não poderá reverter ou reenviar esta submissão por aqui.
   341	      </InlineNotice>
   342	      {mutation.isPending ? <AsyncFeedback state="PENDING" message="Enviando evidência…" /> : null}
   343	      {mutation.isError ? (
   344	        <InlineNotice tone="critical" announce="alert" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => void handleSubmit()}>Tentar novamente</Button>}>
   345	          Não foi possível enviar. Tente novamente.
   346	        </InlineNotice>
   347	      ) : null}
   348	      <Button variant="ghost" disabled={mutation.isPending} onClick={onBack}>
   349	        Voltar
   350	      </Button>{" "}
   351	      <Button variant="primary" pending={mutation.isPending} onClick={() => void handleSubmit()}>
   352	        Enviar evidência
   353	      </Button>
   354	    </div>
   355	  );
   356	}

NOTA: 5,8/10  
VEREDITO: NEEDS FIXES

ACHADOS:

1. **BLOQUEANTE — A proposta pressupõe um `Update` de `DocumentVersion` que não existe.** Em “Design proposto §1”, afirma que “o `Update` já existente em `DocumentVersion` ganha” `fileSetSealed`, `principalFileId`, `totalFiles` e `pendingFileScans`. Hoje a versão é criada por `Put` em [guest-document-access-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/application/guest-document-access-service.ts:415), e o único `Update` da transação é do `DocumentRequest`, na linha 469. Criar a versão por `Put` e atualizá-la na mesma `TransactWriteItems` é inválido porque duas ações não podem atingir o mesmo item. Esses campos precisam nascer diretamente no objeto `version` do `Put`.

2. **BLOQUEANTE — O `DocumentFile` proposto não recebe o ponteiro GSI8 necessário para timeout.** O `Put` descrito em “Design proposto §1” menciona estado, papel e `quarantineObject`, mas omite `deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue()` e `documentFileGsi8Keys()`. O padrão real de `reserveFiles()` grava esses atributos em [document-archive-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/application/document-archive-service.ts:663). O reconciliador descobre arquivos exclusivamente via GSI8; sem esses atributos, o caso “guest nunca faz PUT” não expira, contradizendo diretamente “Frontend §4”.

3. **BLOQUEANTE — Com as flags atualmente desligadas, os eventos físicos são descartados e o arquivo nunca chega a `CLEAN`.** Tanto o finalizador quanto o processador de malware retornam `IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED` antes de ler o arquivo quando `EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED` e `DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED` não estão ambas ativas; veja [document-archive-finalizer.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/workers/upload-finalizer/document-archive-finalizer.ts:50) e [document-archive-result-processor.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/workers/malware-result/document-archive-result-processor.ts:45). Os eventos são descartados, não retomados após ativação. A proposta precisa incluir o sequenciamento de ativação ou desenhar explicitamente como o scanning guest funciona sem acoplar a entrega à ativação de OCR, hoje declarada fora de escopo.

4. **MENOR — Falta validação de `checksumSha256` no serviço.** “Design proposto §1” exige validação em profundidade no serviço, mas enumera somente `mediaType` e `contentLength`. O signer converte o valor com `Buffer.from(..., "hex")`, enquanto o precedente real valida explicitamente 64 caracteres hexadecimais em `DocumentService.reserveUpload()`. Depender apenas do JSON Schema contradiz a disciplina declarada pela própria proposta.

5. **MENOR — O caminho de importação proposto para as funções do frontend não compila.** “Frontend §4” manda importar diretamente `computeChecksumSha256()` e `uploadDocumentBytes()` de `hooks/useUploadDocument.ts`, mas esse módulo apenas importa essas funções; não as exporta. As exportações reais estão em [documents.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/frontend/src/api/documents.ts:34).

6. **MENOR — A promessa de erro genérico não vale na borda HTTP.** O schema é validado antes da resolução da sessão e lança `ValidationError` com os erros do Ajv em [document-archive-guest-handlers.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/http/document-archive-guest-handlers.ts:176). Portanto, media type, tamanho ou checksum inválidos podem produzir detalhes distintos antes de alcançar o colapso para `GuestAccessInvalidError` proposto no serviço. Isso não revela tenant, mas contradiz literalmente a garantia de nunca expor o detalhe da falha ao guest.
