   324	    };
   325	    const created = await this.store.putIfAbsent(session);
   326	    if (!created) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError(); // astronomically unlikely selector collision.
   327	    return { session: issued, expiresAt, subjectDisplayName: resolved.subjectDisplayName, requirementName: resolved.requirementName };
   328	  }
   329	
   330	  /** Resolves a GuestSession token — same parse/rate-limit/lookup/dummy-compare/expiry
   331	   * discipline as `resolveCredential`, over the session's own tenantless namespace. */
   332	  private async resolveSession(rawSessionToken: string, requestContext: { ip: string }): Promise<ResolvedSession> {
   333	    const parsed = parseGuestSessionToken(rawSessionToken);
   334	    if (!parsed) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   335	
   336	    const selectorHash = hmacGuestSessionCrypto.hash(this.pepper, parsed.selector);
   337	    try {
   338	      await this.rateLimiter.consumeBoth({ requestKey: selectorHash, ip: requestContext.ip, limit: RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE, windowSeconds: RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS });
   339	    } catch {
   340	      throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    const pointer = await this.store.get<GuestSession>(guestSessionKey(selectorHash));
   344	    const targetSecretHash = pointer?.secretHash ?? hmacGuestSessionCrypto.hash(this.pepper, `dummy:${selectorHash}`);
   345	    const secretOk = guestSessionSecretMatches(this.pepper, parsed.secret, targetSecretHash);
   346	    if (!pointer || !secretOk) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   347	    if (pointer.expiresAt < this.now()) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();

   350	    if (!request || !isDocumentRequestLive(request.status)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();

   352	    return { session: pointer, request };
   353	  }

   355	  /** Layer 3: idempotent evidence submission. `csrfCookieValue`/`csrfHeaderValue` are the raw
   356	   * double-submit pair — mismatch (missing either side, or the two differing) is a CSRF failure
   357	   * and collapses into the same generic error as every other guest-auth failure mode. */
   358	  async submitEvidence(
   359	    rawSessionToken: string,
   360	    requestContext: { ip: string; csrfCookieValue: string | undefined; csrfHeaderValue: string | undefined },
   361	    input: SubmitEvidenceInput,
   362	  ): Promise<SubmitEvidenceResult> {
   363	    const resolved = await this.resolveSession(rawSessionToken, requestContext);
   364	
   365	    // Double-submit CSRF: cookie and header must both be present AND equal AND match the
   366	    // session's own stored hash (defends both "no cookie at all" and "attacker can set an
   367	    // arbitrary matching pair but doesn't know the real session's csrfToken" — the latter is the
   368	    // whole point of hashing it server-side rather than only comparing cookie===header).
   369	    if (!requestContext.csrfCookieValue || !requestContext.csrfHeaderValue || requestContext.csrfCookieValue !== requestContext.csrfHeaderValue) {

   371	    }

   373	      throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();

   409	      ...documentGsi1Keys(tenantId, "ACTIVE", now, documentId),
   410	      // GSI2 (Documents-by-Subject) — a second attribute set on the same physical row, no
   411	      // mirror item needed (same pattern as DocumentArchiveService.createDocument).
   412	      ...documentGsi2Keys(tenantId, subjectId, documentTypeId, documentId),

   423	      // never auto-accepted — this single transaction goes straight to RECEIVED (the compressed
   424	      // internal-flow precedent for "DRAFT -> RECEIVED in one step", never further to ACCEPTED).
   425	      state: "RECEIVED",

   428	      pendingFileScans: 0,
   429	      infectedFileScans: 0,

   473	          tenantId,
   474	          expectedVersion: resolved.request.version,
   475	          set: { status: "SUBMITTED", lastSubmissionId: versionId, submissionCount: resolved.request.submissionCount + 1 },
   476	          now,
   477	        }),
   478	      },
   479	    ];
   480	
   481	    try {
   482	      await executeTenantBusinessMutation({ store: this.store, tableName: this.tableName, tenantId, entries });
   483	    } catch (err) {
   484	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) {
   485	        const replay = await this.store.get<{ resultSnapshot: SubmitEvidenceResult } & EntityKey>(idempotencyKey);
   486	        if (replay) return replay.resultSnapshot;
   487	        // A genuine race (e.g. two near-simultaneous submits of the same session) or the tenant
   488	        // leaving ACTIVE — the guest never sees which; same anti-enumeration collapse as every
   489	        // other failure mode on this surface.
   490	        throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   491	      }
   492	      if (err instanceof TenantNotActiveError) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   493	      throw err;
   494	    }
   495	
   496	    return result;
   497	  }
   498	
   499	  private async markOpened(request: DocumentRequest): Promise<void> {
   500	    try {
   501	      await this.store.transactWrite([
   502	        {
   503	          Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   504	            tableName: this.tableName,
   505	            key: documentRequestKey(authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(request), request.subjectId, request.documentRequestId),
   506	            tenantId: request.tenantId,
   507	            expectedVersion: request.version,
   508	            set: { status: "OPENED", lastOpenedAt: this.now() },
   509	          }),
   510	        },
   511	      ]);
   512	    } catch (err) {
   513	      // Best-effort — a concurrent read race (two near-simultaneous opens of the same link)
   514	      // must never block the guest from seeing the request's info.
   515	      if (!isTransactionCanceled(err)) throw err;
   516	    }
   517	  }
   518	}
     1	/**
     2	 * D-193 item 8/9 ("Sequenciamento", `estado-final-consolidado.md`) — the mandatory-order
     3	 * activation gate for the `document-archive` OCR/extraction pipeline (D-193 slices 1-7). Two
     4	 * independent AppConfig flags, `EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED` (STARTER) and
     5	 * `DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED` (PROMOTER), activate in a mandatory order — STARTER
     6	 * strictly before PROMOTER, never the reverse — so that a `document-archive` file is never
     7	 * promoted to the CLEAN key while nothing is listening to start extraction against it (the
     8	 * "CLEAN sem consumidor" window the approved design named explicitly).
     9	 *
    10	 * A runbook note alone ("turn on STARTER first, then PROMOTER") only prevents the wrong order
    11	 * when the operator follows it. This function closes the same window BY CONSTRUCTION instead:
    12	 * the promoter path (`upload-finalizer-handler.ts`/`malware-result-handler.ts`'s third branch,
    13	 * reaching `applyFileScanResult`/`confirmFileScanClean`) is gated on THIS function, not on the
    14	 * raw `DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED` value alone — so turning PROMOTION on while STARTER
    15	 * is still off (the forbidden reverse order) can never activate promotion. There is then no code
    16	 * path that produces a CLEAN `document-archive` object while its consumer (the Starter) is off.
    17	 */
    18	import type { FeatureFlags } from "../ports/feature-flags-reader.js";
    19	
    20	/** STARTER alone gates whether `startExtractionRunForDocumentArchive()` may open the
    21	 * ExtractionRun gate / call `startExecution()`. No dependency on PROMOTER — the Starter only
    22	 * ever reacts to an ALREADY-clean object (D-193's own "never trusts the S3 event, always rereads
    23	 * DocumentFile fresh" discipline), so it needs no cross-flag ordering guard of its own. */
    24	export function isDocumentArchiveExtractionTriggerEnabled(flags: FeatureFlags): boolean {
    25	  return flags.EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED === true;

    30	 * must treat a `document-archive` quarantine key exactly like an unrecognized key shape: logged

    32	export function isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled(flags: FeatureFlags): boolean {
    33	  return flags.EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED === true && flags.DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED === true;
    34	}

    76	  async reserveUpload(ctx: RequestContext, itemId: string, input: ReserveUploadInput, idempotencyKey: string): Promise<ReserveUploadResult> {
    77	    authorize({ context: ctx, action: "document:reserve-upload", resource: { tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId } });
    78	
    79	    if (!ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.has(input.mediaType)) {
    80	      throw new ValidationError("Unsupported media type.", { mediaType: input.mediaType });
    81	    }
    82	    if (input.contentLength <= 0 || input.contentLength > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) {
    83	      throw new ValidationError("contentLength must be between 1 and 10MiB.", { contentLength: input.contentLength, maxBytes: MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES });
    84	    }
    85	    if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(input.checksumSha256)) {
    86	      throw new ValidationError("checksumSha256 must be a 64-character hex SHA-256 digest.");
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    const requestHash = `${itemId}|${input.fileName}|${input.mediaType}|${input.contentLength}|${input.checksumSha256}`;
    90	    const now = this.now();
    91	    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.parse(now) + PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS * 1000).toISOString();
    92	
    93	    const begin = await this.idempotency.begin({
    94	      tenantId: ctx.tenant.tenantId,

    97	      requestHash,
    98	      expiresAt,
    99	    });

    29	/** Phase 2 of the two-phase model - a direct-to-storage PUT, deliberately NOT going through
    30	 * `apiClient` (no BFF credentials/CSRF apply to a presigned URL; it is its own auth). Failure
    31	 * here leaves the document in `PENDING_UPLOAD` forever from the backend's point of view until
    32	 * `UploadSlotReconciliationWorker` eventually times it out - the UI's job is only to surface
    33	 * that failure honestly, never to pretend the reservation alone means the file is attached. */
    34	export async function uploadDocumentBytes(uploadUrl: string, requiredHeaders: Record<string, string>, file: File): Promise<void> {
    35	  const response = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: requiredHeaders, body: file });
    36	  if (!response.ok) {
    37	    throw new Error(`Upload failed with status ${response.status}`);
    38	  }
    39	}
    40	
    41	/** SHA-256 checksum the backend's `ReserveUploadInput.checksumSha256` requires up front (the
    42	 * reservation call happens BEFORE any bytes are sent, so this is computed client-side from the
    43	 * File object itself, not received from a server round-trip). */
    44	function readAsArrayBuffer(file: File): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
    45	  // FileReader rather than `file.arrayBuffer()`/`new Response(file).arrayBuffer()` - both of
    46	  // those are unreliable in this project's jsdom test environment (real browsers implement
    47	  // both fine, but neither actually reads the File's bytes under jsdom - a real gap found
    48	  // while writing this function's own test), while FileReader is well-supported everywhere.
    49	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    50	    const reader = new FileReader();
    51	    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as ArrayBuffer);
    52	    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error ?? new Error("FileReader failed."));
    53	    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    54	  });
    55	}
    56	
    57	export async function computeChecksumSha256(file: File): Promise<string> {
    58	  const buffer = await readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    59	  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buffer);
    60	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
    61	    .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    62	    .join("");
    63	}

     2	 * HTTP handlers for /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}* — D-143 Decision 4.

    20	const GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_session";

    24	interface CookieAttributes {
    25	  httpOnly: boolean;
    26	  maxAgeSeconds: number;
    27	}
    28	
    29	function buildSetCookieHeader(name: string, value: string, attrs: CookieAttributes): string {
    30	  const parts = [`${name}=${value}`, "Path=/", `Max-Age=${attrs.maxAgeSeconds}`, "SameSite=Strict", "Secure"];
    31	  if (attrs.httpOnly) parts.push("HttpOnly");
    32	  return parts.join("; ");
    33	}
    34	
    35	function parseCookieHeader(header: string | undefined): Record<string, string> {
    36	  if (!header) return {};
    37	  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    38	  for (const pair of header.split(";")) {
    39	    const eq = pair.indexOf("=");
    40	    if (eq === -1) continue;
    41	    const name = pair.slice(0, eq).trim();
    42	    const value = pair.slice(eq + 1).trim();
    43	    if (name) out[name] = value;
    44	  }
    45	  return out;
    46	}
    47	
    48	function validateAgainstSchema(schemaId: string, body: unknown): void {
    49	  const { valid, errors } = defaultSchemaRegistry.validate(schemaId, body);
    50	  if (!valid) throw new ValidationError("Request body failed schema validation.", { errors });
    51	}

    73	const STATUS_BY_CATEGORY: Record<string, number> = {
    74	  VALIDATION: 400,
    75	  AUTH: 401,
    76	  AUTHORIZATION: 403,
    77	  NOT_FOUND: 404,
    78	  CONFLICT: 409,
    79	  QUOTA_EXCEEDED: 429,
    80	  DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE: 503,
    81	  INTERNAL: 500,
    82	};

    84	/** Every response from this handler set carries `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` — Decision 4. */
    85	function baseHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
    86	  return { "content-type": "application/json", "referrer-policy": "no-referrer" };
    87	}
    88	
    89	function toResponse(appError: AppError): GuestArchiveHttpResponse {
    90	  const status = STATUS_BY_CATEGORY[appError.category] ?? 500;
    91	  return { statusCode: status, headers: baseHeaders(), body: appError.toJSON() };
    92	}

    94	async function withErrorMapping(fn: () => Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse>): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
    95	  try {
    96	    return await fn();
    97	  } catch (err) {
    98	    return toResponse(err instanceof AppError ? err : toAppError(err));
    99	  }
   100	}

   102	function requireToken(req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): string {
   103	  const token = req.pathParameters?.["token"];
   104	  if (!token) throw new ValidationError("Missing token path parameter.");
   105	  return token;
   106	}

   108	function cookiesOf(req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Record<string, string> {
   109	  return parseCookieHeader(req.headers?.["cookie"] ?? req.headers?.["Cookie"]);
   110	}

   175	
   176	export async function handleSubmitEvidence(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest<SubmitEvidenceInput>): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
   177	  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
   178	    requireToken(req); // Presence-checked for route symmetry/observability; resolution is by session cookie, not the path token.
   179	    // Pre-existing gap, found (not introduced) by Codex review round 1 of D-264 (Block 6, G02
   180	    // frontend): the session cookie (GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME) is a single global cookie per
   181	    // browser, never bound to/verified against THIS route's own `token` path segment. Two guest
   182	    // links open in two tabs of the same browser share one cookie jar - the second `session`
   183	    // mint overwrites the first tab's cookie, so the first tab's later `uploads` call resolves
   184	    // against the SECOND session/DocumentRequest, not its own. Real since D-146 (a single-link-
   185	    // at-a-time design); only now reachable via a real multi-tab UI. Fixing this means binding
   186	    // the session to its issuing token (or scoping the cookie name by token) - a session-identity
   187	    // design change that deserves its own scoping, not a rushed patch. Named in decisions-log
   188	    // D-264.
   189	    if (!req.body) throw new ValidationError("Missing request body.");
   190	    validateAgainstSchema(SUBMIT_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA_ID, req.body);
   191	
   192	    const cookies = cookiesOf(req);
   193	    const sessionToken = cookies[GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME];
   194	    // A missing session cookie is itself a guest-auth failure mode (no session to resolve) —
   195	    // collapses into the same generic error as a malformed/expired/wrong-secret token, never a
   196	    // differentiated 400 that would tell a caller "you're just missing a cookie" (an oracle).
   197	    if (!sessionToken) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   198	    const csrfHeaderValue = req.headers?.[CSRF_HEADER_NAME] ?? req.headers?.["X-CSRF-Token"];
   199	
   200	    const result = await deps.guestAccess.submitEvidence(
   201	      sessionToken,
   202	      { ip: req.sourceIp, csrfCookieValue: cookies[GUEST_CSRF_COOKIE_NAME], csrfHeaderValue },
   203	      req.body,
   204	    );
   205	    return { statusCode: 201, headers: baseHeaders(), body: result as unknown as Record<string, unknown> };
   206	  });
   207	}

     1	/**
     2	 * DocumentRequest — document-archive's OWN minimal shape, D-143 Decision 4 (guest access). NOT
     3	 * the older `src/modules/subject/domain/document-request.ts` (that entity belongs to the
     4	 * subject module's M10 guest-upload slice, keyed under `TENANT#t#SUBJECT#s`/
     5	 * `REQASSIGN#<assignmentId>#DOCREQ#<id>` and carrying its own `tokenSelectorHash` inline) — this
     6	 * is a distinct, new entity scoped to the document-archive domain (Document/DocumentVersion/
     7	 * Requirement), the one `estado-final-consolidado.md` Decision 8 (recurrence) references via
     8	 * `requestId`/`attemptIndex`/`seriesId`.
     9	 *
    10	 * Deliberately minimal: this task (guest access, D-143 Decision 4) only needs "the business
    11	 * request a RequestAccessCredential is issued against" — id/subjectId/tenantId/requirementId/
    12	 * status/deadline. Recurrence (Decision 8, D-147) ADDS `seriesId`/`occurrenceId`/
    13	 * `attemptIndex`/`parentRequestId` below rather than introducing a parallel entity.
    14	 *
    15	 * D-147 (Decision 8, recurrence): all four new fields are OPTIONAL/additive — a bare
    16	 * `DocumentRequest` created outside a series (the only kind guest access's existing tests
    17	 * construct) simply omits them, same "no fabricated value" discipline as every other optional
    18	 * field in this module (`requirement.ts`'s `evidenceValidUntil`, etc.). `attemptIndex` is the
    19	 * one exception worth calling out: it defaults to `1` for a non-recurring request (see
    20	 * `document-request-series.ts`'s `materializeAttempt`), so it is typed as a required `number`
    21	 * with `1` as the implicit non-recurring value, not `attemptIndex?: number` — every
    22	 * DocumentRequest that exists IS exactly one attempt, whether or not it belongs to a series.
    23	 */
    24	import type { EntityKey } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/occ.js";
    25	import type { AuthorizedTenantId } from "../../identity/domain/authorization.js";
    26	
    27	/** Mirrors the older subject-module DocumentRequest's status vocabulary (same states cover the
    28	 * same real lifecycle — requested/opened/submitted/completed, plus the three ways it can die
    29	 * early) — not reused by import (distinct module, distinct entity), just the same taxonomy. */
    30	export type DocumentRequestStatus = "REQUESTED" | "OPENED" | "SUBMITTED" | "COMPLETED" | "CANCELLED" | "EXPIRED" | "REVOKED";
    31	
    32	export interface DocumentRequest extends EntityKey {
    33	  SK: `DOCREQUEST#${string}`;
    34	  entityType: "DocumentRequest";
    35	  documentRequestId: string;
    36	  tenantId: string;
    37	  subjectId: string;
    38	  /** The Requirement this request asks the guest to satisfy — a request always targets exactly
    39	   * one Requirement in this minimal shape (no fan-out to multiple Requirements per request). */
    40	  requirementId: string;
    41	  status: DocumentRequestStatus;
    42	  /** Also the RequestAccessCredential's TTL (Decision 4: "TTL = prazo do Request de negócio") —
    43	   * absent means no deadline, in which case credential issuance must supply an explicit TTL of
    44	   * its own rather than defaulting to "forever" (see `issueRequestAccessCredential`). */
    45	  deadline?: string;
    46	  lastOpenedAt?: string;
    47	  lastSubmissionId?: string;
    48	  submissionCount: number;
    49	  /** D-147 (Decision 8): present only when this request belongs to a `DocumentRequestSeries`
    50	   * cycle — see `document-request-series.ts` for the series/cycle/attempt shape. */
    51	  seriesId?: string;
    52	  /** Deterministic per-cycle id (`computeSeriesOccurrenceId`) — stable across every attempt of
    53	   * the SAME cycle, changes only when the series advances to its next cycle. Never present
    54	   * without `seriesId`. */
    55	  occurrenceId?: string;
    56	  /** Which attempt within the cycle this is — `1` for a series' first attempt of a cycle,
    57	   * incrementing on each `materializeAttempt` call within the same cycle. Optional (not
    58	   * defaulted to `1`) rather than required: a bare, non-recurring `DocumentRequest` (guest
    59	   * access's existing shape, no `seriesId`) has no cycle at all, so "attempt 1 of what?" does
    60	   * not apply to it — forcing a fabricated `1` onto every non-recurring request would violate
    61	   * this module's "no fabricated value" discipline (see `requirement.ts`'s `evidenceValidUntil`
    62	   * for the same principle applied elsewhere). Always present together with `seriesId`. */
    63	  attemptIndex?: number;
    64	  /** Always the immediately-previous attempt of the SAME cycle (never the previous cycle's
    65	   * last attempt) — preserves causality within a cycle while `occurrenceId` preserves cycle
    66	   * identity across attempts (D-147/Decision 8). Absent for attempt 1 of any cycle. */
    67	  parentRequestId?: string;
    68	  /**
    69	   * D-226 (`guest-credential-issuance-scoping/estado-final-consolidado.md`): monotonic counter,
    70	   * starts at `1` at creation, incremented by `rejectVersion()` on every reissue-triggering
    71	   * rejection (Achado 3 of D-222). Identity for credential ISSUANCE is `documentRequestId` +
    72	   * `issuanceGeneration` together, never `documentRequestId` alone — this is what lets a
    73	   * post-rejection reissue avoid colliding with the original issuance's idempotency record on
    74	   * the guest Lambda's consumer, and what the consumer's `DocumentRequest` `Update` fences on
    75	   * (`ConditionExpression` includes `issuanceGeneration = :expected`) to reject a stale/obsolete
    76	   * issuance event without a TOCTOU window.
    77	   */
    78	  issuanceGeneration: number;
    79	  /**
    80	   * D-226: selector hash of the currently-active `RequestAccessCredential` for this request —
    81	   * written ONLY by the guest-Lambda issuance consumer (the one holding the D-146 pepper), never
    82	   * by this (authenticated, pepper-less) Lambda. Its purpose is to let THIS Lambda revoke a
    83	   * stale credential (`rejectVersion()`) by a known key, without ever needing to compute or
    84	   * verify a hash itself — revocation is a plain conditional `SET revokedAt`, no pepper required.
    85	   */
    86	  activeCredentialSelectorHash?: string;
    87	  /**
    88	   * D-226 (Achado 3 of D-222): set by `rejectVersion()` when it reopens this request — one
    89	   * object, never two loose fields, so the reason always stays correlated to the exact version
    90	   * and moment it was rejected (never an ambiguous "last reason" disconnected from which
    91	   * submission it belonged to).
    92	   */
    93	  lastRejection?: { versionId: string; reason: string; occurredAt: string };
    94	  /**
    95	   * D-228 (closes D-222/D-227's named gap: this entity had no way to know WHO to deliver a
    96	   * guest link to). Optional/additive — same "no fabricated value" discipline as every other
    97	   * optional field here — following the exact precedent already established for the SAME
    98	   * problem (delivering a guest link to an external party) in the older, unrelated
    99	   * `src/modules/subject/domain/document-request.ts`'s `recipientEmail: string` (there
   100	   * required, because that entity's ONLY creation path — `CreateDocumentRequestInput` —
   101	   * already demands it; here optional, because a series-materialized attempt
   102	   * (`document-request-series.ts`'s `materializeAttempt`) has no recipient contact modeled at
   103	   * the series level yet — a genuine product gap named, not solved, by this decision; see
   104	   * decisions-log.md D-228). Populated only by `createDocumentRequest()`'s avulso path today.
   105	   * The delivery worker (`src/workers/guest-credential-delivery/deliver.ts`) treats its
   106	   * absence as a terminal, non-retryable skip rather than an error — a request with no
   107	   * recipient is a data gap, not a transient failure.
   108	   */
   109	  recipientEmail?: string;
   110	  createdAt: string;
   111	  updatedAt: string;
   112	  version: number;
   113	}
   114	
   115	export function documentRequestKey(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, subjectId: string, documentRequestId: string): { PK: string; SK: `DOCREQUEST#${string}` } {
   116	  return { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#SUBJECT#${subjectId}`, SK: `DOCREQUEST#${documentRequestId}` };
   117	}

   119	export const DOCUMENT_REQUEST_SK_PREFIX = "DOCREQUEST#";
   120	
   121	/** States a resolved credential/session may still act against — mirrors
   122	 * `GuestSubmissionService.resolveToken`'s terminal-status rejection list (subject module
   123	 * precedent) applied to this module's own status vocabulary. */
   124	export function isDocumentRequestLive(status: DocumentRequestStatus): boolean {
   125	  return status !== "CANCELLED" && status !== "REVOKED" && status !== "EXPIRED" && status !== "COMPLETED";
   126	}
    45	
    46	export interface ApplyFileScanResultInput {
    47	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
    48	  documentId: string;
    49	  seq: number;
    50	  fileId: string;
    51	  /** The object reference THIS physical event actually observed (S3 Object Created / GuardDuty
    52	   * finding) - never the stored `DocumentFile.quarantineObject`, per D-163 §1: the first event
    53	   * to arrive consolidates FROM this triple, the second is verified AGAINST what got
    54	   * consolidated, never re-derived from the Version or fabricated. */
    55	  observedObject: DocumentObjectReference;
    56	  uploadEvidence?: UploadEvidence;
    57	  malwareEvidence?: MalwareEvidence;
    58	}
    59	
    60	export type ApplyFileScanResultOutcome =
    61	  | { outcome: "AWAITING" }
    62	  | { outcome: "IGNORED_WRONG_VERSION" }
    63	  | { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" }
    64	  | { outcome: "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE" }
    65	  | { outcome: "REJECTED"; status: Extract<DocumentFileScanStatus, "REJECTED" | "UNSUPPORTED"> }
    66	  | { outcome: "READY_TO_PROMOTE"; sourceObject: DocumentObjectReference };
    67	
    68	const MAX_OCC_RETRIES = 10;
    69	
    70	/** Same get-or-create shape as `DocumentArchiveService.ensureStorageQuota()` (kept as a free
    71	 * function here since this module's functions take `deps`, not a class instance) — storage-
    72	 * quota-scoping (D-2xx). */
    73	async function ensureStorageQuota(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId): Promise<TenantStorageQuota> {
    74	  const key = storageQuotaKey(tenantId);
    75	  const existing = await deps.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
    76	  if (existing) return existing;
    77	  const created = defaultStorageQuota(tenantId, (deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString()))());
    78	  const wrote = await deps.store.putIfAbsent(created);
    79	  if (wrote) return created;
    80	  const fresh = await deps.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
    81	  if (!fresh) throw new Error(`Storage quota record vanished after creation race for tenant ${tenantId}.`);
    82	  return fresh;
    83	}
    84	
    85	/** Re-reads the file fresh on every attempt - never assumes the caller's in-memory copy is
    86	 * current, since the upload-finalizer and malware-scan events for the SAME file may land
    87	 * concurrently, exactly the corridor `decideNextAction()`'s doc comment describes for M6. */
    88	export async function applyFileScanResult(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, input: ApplyFileScanResultInput): Promise<ApplyFileScanResultOutcome> {
    89	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
    90	  const key = documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId);
    91	
    92	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
    93	    const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
    94	    if (!file) return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" };
    95	
    96	    // Terminal-state idempotency FIRST, before any correlation check: a repeated/late event
    97	    // against a file already at CLEAN/REJECTED/UNSUPPORTED/TIMEOUT is a legitimate replay, never
    98	    // re-evaluated against the consolidated triple (D-163 §1's correlation only ever applies
    99	    // while `scanStatus IN (PENDING_UPLOAD, SCANNING)`).
   100	    if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" };
   101	
   102	    // D-163 §1 symmetric evidence correlation: `quarantineObject.versionId === ""` means no
   103	    // physical event has consolidated it yet (`reserveFiles()`'s placeholder) - this event IS
   104	    // the first, so it wins unconditionally. Once consolidated, every further event must match
   105	    // that exact triple or is a stale/duplicate/wrong-object event, never re-consolidated.
   106	    const consolidated = file.quarantineObject.versionId !== "";
   107	    if (consolidated && !sameObjectVersion(file.quarantineObject, input.observedObject)) {
   108	      return { outcome: "IGNORED_WRONG_VERSION" };
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    const mergedUploadEvidence = input.uploadEvidence ?? file.uploadEvidence;
   112	    const mergedMalwareEvidence = input.malwareEvidence ?? file.malwareEvidence;
   113	
   114	    const decision = decideNextAction({
   115	      currentStatus: file.scanStatus,
   116	      uploadValid: mergedUploadEvidence?.valid,
   117	      uploadEvidence: mergedUploadEvidence,
   118	      malwareEvidence: mergedMalwareEvidence,
   119	    });
   120	
   121	    const evidenceSet: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   122	    if (input.uploadEvidence) evidenceSet["uploadEvidence"] = input.uploadEvidence;
   123	    if (input.malwareEvidence) evidenceSet["malwareEvidence"] = input.malwareEvidence;
   124	    const quarantineObjectSet = consolidated ? {} : { quarantineObject: input.observedObject };
   125	
   126	    if (decision.action === "IGNORE_STALE_EVENT") return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" };
   127	
   128	    if (decision.action === "AWAIT_MORE_EVIDENCE") {
   129	      const nowTs = now();
   130	      const result = await tryTenantBusinessMutation({
   131	        store: deps.store,
   132	        tableName: deps.tableName,
   133	        tenantId: input.tenantId,
   134	        entries: [
   135	          {
   136	            Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   137	              tableName: deps.tableName,
   138	              key,
   139	              tenantId: input.tenantId,
   140	              expectedVersion: file.version,
   141	              // SCANNING marks "at least one physical event has landed" independent of
   142	              // decideNextAction's own vocabulary (it only ever returns actions, never this
   143	              // specific status) - a no-op re-SET when the file was already SCANNING.
   144	              set: { scanStatus: "SCANNING", ...quarantineObjectSet, ...evidenceSet },
   145	              now: nowTs,
   146	            }),
   147	          },
   148	        ],
   149	      });
   150	      if (result.ok) return { outcome: "AWAITING" };
   151	      if (result.reason === "OCC_CONFLICT") continue; // concurrent half of evidence landed - retry fresh.
   152	      return { outcome: "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE" };
   153	    }
   154	
   155	    if (decision.action === "PROMOTE") {
   156	      // Never claims CLEAN itself - zero writes here, same discipline M6's PROMOTE branch
   157	      // established (`advance-after-evidence.ts`'s doc comment). The evidence merge that got us
   158	      // here was already persisted by an earlier AWAITING call (PROMOTE is only reachable once
   159	      // BOTH evidences exist, and only one arrives per call) - nothing new to consolidate.
   160	      const sourceObject = consolidated ? file.quarantineObject : input.observedObject;
   161	      return { outcome: "READY_TO_PROMOTE", sourceObject };
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    // decision.action === "REJECT": terminal transition - counters + GSI8 removal happen in the
   165	    // SAME transaction as the file's own write (Decision 6/Bloqueador 9: a crash between the two
   166	    // would leave pendingFileScans stuck above zero forever, permanently blocking acceptVersion).
   167	    const version = await deps.store.get<DocumentVersion>(documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq));
   168	    if (!version) return { outcome: "IGNORED_STALE" }; // Version cannot be removed once files exist - fail closed if this ever changes.
   169	
   170	    const infected = mergedMalwareEvidence?.status === "THREATS_FOUND";
   171	    const nowTs = now();
   172	    // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): REJECTED/UNSUPPORTED is terminal without ever reaching
   173	    // CLEAN, so the capacity hold `reserveFiles()` took on this file's bytes must be released
   174	    // here — in the SAME transaction as the file's own terminal write, same "counters change
   175	    // atomically with the state transition, never a separate step" discipline this file's
   176	    // pendingFileScans decrement already follows (module doc comment, Decision 6/Bloqueador 9).
   177	    const quota = await ensureStorageQuota(deps, input.tenantId);
   178	    const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   179	      {
   180	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   181	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   182	          key,
   183	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   184	          expectedVersion: file.version,
   185	          set: { scanStatus: decision.status, ...quarantineObjectSet, ...evidenceSet },
   186	          // D-163 §5/§6: any transaction reaching a terminal scanStatus removes the sparse GSI8
   187	          // reconciliation pointer in the same write - never a separate cleanup step.
   188	          remove: ["GSI8PK", "GSI8SK"],
   189	          now: nowTs,
   190	        }),
   191	      },
   192	      {
   193	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   194	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   195	          key: documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq),
   196	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   197	          expectedVersion: version.version,
   198	          set: { pendingFileScans: version.pendingFileScans - 1, ...(infected ? { infectedFileScans: version.infectedFileScans + 1 } : {}) },
   199	          now: nowTs,
   200	        }),
   201	      },
   202	      {
   203	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   204	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   205	          key: storageQuotaKey(input.tenantId),
   206	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   207	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   208	          set: { reservedBytes: Math.max(0, quota.reservedBytes - file.contentLength) },
   209	          now: nowTs,
   210	        }),
   211	      },
   212	    ];
   213	    // D-163 §2 (Rodada 4): FILE_REJECTED_INFECTED is emitted only for the infection-caused
   214	    // REJECTED transition, never for a plain upload-invalid REJECTED or an UNSUPPORTED one - the
   215	    // audit trail exists specifically so "why was this file removed from consideration" is
   216	    // answerable without re-deriving it from malwareEvidence.
   217	    if (infected) {
   218	      entries.push({
   219	        Put: buildVersionedCreate(
   220	          deps.tableName,
   221	          buildFileRejectedEvent(deps, input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, version.versionId, version.state, input.fileId, file.scanStatus, decision.status, nowTs) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey,
   222	        ),
   223	      });
   224	    }
   225	    const result = await tryTenantBusinessMutation({ store: deps.store, tableName: deps.tableName, tenantId: input.tenantId, entries });
   226	    if (result.ok) return { outcome: "REJECTED", status: decision.status };
   227	    if (result.reason === "OCC_CONFLICT") continue;
   228	    return { outcome: "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE" };
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  throw new Error(`applyFileScanResult exhausted retries for file ${input.fileId} under contention.`);
   232	}
   233	
   234	export type ConfirmFileScanCleanOutcome = "CONFIRMED" | "IGNORED_STALE" | "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE";
   235	
   236	export interface ConfirmFileScanCleanInput {
   237	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
   238	  documentId: string;
   239	  seq: number;
   240	  fileId: string;
   241	  cleanObject: DocumentObjectReference;
   242	}
   243	
   244	/**
   245	 * confirmFileScanClean - the PROMOTE confirmation step, called only once the caller (a future
   246	 * S3/GuardDuty event worker, out of scope for this slice) has already copied `sourceObject` to
   247	 * `cleanObject` and verified it (size/checksum, same discipline M6's `advanceAfterEvidence()`
   248	 * requires before it will persist CLEAN). Idempotent against a file that already moved off
   249	 * SCANNING/PENDING_UPLOAD by the time this is called (e.g. concurrently rejected) - never
   250	 * resurrects a terminal file back toward CLEAN.
   251	 */
   252	export async function confirmFileScanClean(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, input: ConfirmFileScanCleanInput): Promise<ConfirmFileScanCleanOutcome> {
   253	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
   254	  const key = documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId);
   255	
   256	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   257	    const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
   258	    if (!file) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   259	    if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   260	
   261	    const version = await deps.store.get<DocumentVersion>(documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq));
   262	    if (!version) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   263	
   264	    const nowTs = now();
   265	    // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): the moment a file durably becomes CLEAN is the real
   266	    // "commit" point for storage — moves `file.contentLength` from `reservedBytes` to
   267	    // `usedBytes` (net zero to the committed total `reserveFiles()` already checked), in the
   268	    // SAME transaction as the CLEAN transition, same discipline as the pendingFileScans entry
   269	    // beside it.
   270	    const quota = await ensureStorageQuota(deps, input.tenantId);
   271	    const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   272	      {
   273	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   274	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   275	          key,
   276	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   277	          expectedVersion: file.version,
   278	          set: { scanStatus: "CLEAN", cleanObject: input.cleanObject },
   279	          remove: ["GSI8PK", "GSI8SK"],
   280	          now: nowTs,
   281	        }),
   282	      },
   283	      {
   284	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   285	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   286	          key: documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq),
   287	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   288	          expectedVersion: version.version,
   289	          set: { pendingFileScans: version.pendingFileScans - 1 },
   290	          now: nowTs,
   291	        }),
   292	      },
   293	      {
   294	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   295	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   296	          key: storageQuotaKey(input.tenantId),
   297	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   298	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   299	          set: { reservedBytes: Math.max(0, quota.reservedBytes - file.contentLength), usedBytes: quota.usedBytes + file.contentLength },
   300	          now: nowTs,
   301	        }),
   302	      },
   303	    ];
   304	    const result = await tryTenantBusinessMutation({ store: deps.store, tableName: deps.tableName, tenantId: input.tenantId, entries });
   305	    if (result.ok) return "CONFIRMED";
   306	    if (result.reason === "OCC_CONFLICT") continue;
   307	    return "IGNORED_TENANT_NOT_ACTIVE";
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  throw new Error(`confirmFileScanClean exhausted retries for file ${input.fileId} under contention.`);
   311	}
   312	
   313	export type ApplyFileScanTimeoutOutcome = "TIMED_OUT" | "IGNORED_STALE";
   314	
   315	export interface ApplyFileScanTimeoutInput {
   316	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId;
   317	  documentId: string;
   318	  seq: number;
   319	  fileId: string;
   320	  /** The exact GSI8 pointer the reconciliation scan observed (D-163 round4 §3) - the
   321	   * transaction below conditions on it verbatim, so a candidate whose deadline changed or
   322	   * that already reached a terminal state between the scan and this write is naturally
   323	   * skipped (ConditionalCheckFailed -> IGNORED_STALE) rather than double-processed. */
   324	  observedGsi8Pointer: { GSI8PK: string; GSI8SK: string };
   325	}
   326	
   327	/**
   328	 * applyFileScanTimeout - the reconciliation worker's terminal transition
   329	 * (PENDING_UPLOAD/SCANNING -> TIMEOUT), symmetric to applyFileScanResult's REJECT branch but
   330	 * reached by deadline rather than by a physical S3/GuardDuty event. Deliberately reuses the
   331	 * exact same counter/GSI8-removal mechanism (buildVersionedUpdate, no new pattern) - the only
   332	 * new ingredient is conditioning on the observed GSI8 pointer itself, which a physical-event
   333	 * caller never needs (it always re-reads the file fresh and checks scanStatus, never a stale
   334	 * discovery pointer).
   335	 */
   336	export async function applyFileScanTimeout(deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps, input: ApplyFileScanTimeoutInput): Promise<ApplyFileScanTimeoutOutcome> {
   337	  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
   338	  const key = documentFileKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq, input.fileId);
   339	
   340	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   341	    const file = await deps.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
   342	    if (!file) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   343	    if (!isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return "IGNORED_STALE";
   344	
   345	    const version = await deps.store.get<DocumentVersion>(documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq));
   346	    if (!version) return "IGNORED_STALE"; // Version cannot be removed once files exist - fail closed if this ever changes.
   347	
   348	    const nowTs = now();
   349	    // storage-quota-scoping (D-2xx): TIMEOUT is terminal without ever reaching CLEAN, same
   350	    // reasoning as the REJECT branch above — release the capacity hold this file's bytes took.
   351	    const quota = await ensureStorageQuota(deps, input.tenantId);
   352	    const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   353	      {
   354	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   355	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   356	          key,
   357	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   358	          expectedVersion: file.version,
   359	          set: { scanStatus: "TIMEOUT" },
   360	          remove: ["GSI8PK", "GSI8SK"],
   361	          // Exact-pointer fence (round4-claude-final.md §3): closes the race where the
   362	          // candidate this scan observed already advanced (new deadline, or terminal) by the
   363	          // time this write lands - never conditioned on `file.version` alone, since a
   364	          // concurrent SCANNING->TIMEOUT-eligible re-write could bump version without changing
   365	          // eligibility in a way the scan already accounted for.
   366	          extraConditions: [
   367	            { expression: "#gsi8pk = :gsi8pk", names: { "#gsi8pk": "GSI8PK" }, values: { ":gsi8pk": input.observedGsi8Pointer.GSI8PK } },
   368	            { expression: "#gsi8sk = :gsi8sk", names: { "#gsi8sk": "GSI8SK" }, values: { ":gsi8sk": input.observedGsi8Pointer.GSI8SK } },
   369	          ],
   370	          now: nowTs,
   371	        }),
   372	      },
   373	      {
   374	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   375	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   376	          key: documentVersionKey(input.tenantId, input.documentId, input.seq),
   377	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   378	          expectedVersion: version.version,
   379	          set: { pendingFileScans: version.pendingFileScans - 1 },
   380	          now: nowTs,
   381	        }),
   382	      },
   383	      {
   384	        Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
   385	          tableName: deps.tableName,
   386	          key: storageQuotaKey(input.tenantId),
   387	          tenantId: input.tenantId,
   388	          expectedVersion: quota.version,
   389	          set: { reservedBytes: Math.max(0, quota.reservedBytes - file.contentLength) },
   390	          now: nowTs,
   391	        }),
   392	      },
   393	    ];
   394	    try {
   395	      await deps.store.transactWrite(entries);
   396	      return "TIMED_OUT";
   397	    } catch (err) {
   398	      // A cancellation here may be the GSI8-pointer race the comment above describes, OR a
   399	      // concurrent write to the quota row (e.g. another file's REJECT/CLEAN transition landing
   400	      // at the same instant) - both are legitimately retried by the reconciliation worker's next
   401	      // sweep (it re-derives GSI8 candidates fresh each run), so IGNORED_STALE is correct for
   402	      // either cause, never a silent quota-accounting drop.
   403	      if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) return "IGNORED_STALE"; // lost the race - a concurrent event/sweep already claimed this file.
   404	      throw err;
   405	    }
   406	  }
   407	
   408	  throw new Error(`applyFileScanTimeout exhausted retries for file ${input.fileId} under contention.`);
   409	}
   410	
   411	function buildFileRejectedEvent(
   412	  deps: ApplyFileScanResultDeps,
   413	  tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId,
   414	  documentId: string,
   415	  seq: number,
   416	  versionId: string,
   417	  versionState: DocumentVersionEvent["toState"],
   418	  fileId: string,
   419	  fromFileScanStatus: DocumentFileScanStatus,
   420	  toFileScanStatus: DocumentFileScanStatus,
   421	  occurredAt: string,
   422	): DocumentVersionEvent {
   423	  return {
   424	    ...documentVersionEventKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, deps.ids.newEventId()),
   425	    entityType: "DocumentVersionEvent",
   426	    tenantId,
   427	    documentId,
   428	    versionId,
   429	    type: "FILE_REJECTED_INFECTED",
   430	    // `fromState`/`toState` record the Version's own (unchanged) state as informational context
   360	    // with no evidence linked) — included anyway for the same uniform-write-site discipline the
   361	    // other 3 mutation sites need, since a future caller shape could change that invariant.
   362	    ...buildRequirementGsi8Fields(status, undefined, tenantId, requirementId),
   363	  };
   364	
   365	  const labels: TransactEntryLabel[] = [{ kind: "REQUIREMENT" }, { kind: "POINTER", name }, { kind: "SUBJECT_FENCE" }];
   366	  const entries: TransactWriteEntry[] = [
   367	    { Put: buildVersionedCreate(tableName, requirement as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
   368	    { Put: buildVersionedCreate(tableName, buildRequirementNamePointer(tenantId, subjectId, name, requirementId, now) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
   369	    buildSubjectFence(tableName, tenantId, subjectId),
   370	  ];
   371	  return { requirement, entries, labels };
   372	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Idempotency-key lifecycle (mission §33-35): one logical user submission = one key. A retry
     3	 * of the SAME submission (the mutation function throwing, user clicks "try again" without
     4	 * changing anything) reuses the SAME key - only calling `newIntent()` (after a genuine
     5	 * success, or when the user deliberately starts a new/different submission) generates a
     6	 * fresh one. Never regenerate on every call, which would silently defeat the backend's
     7	 * idempotency protection (src/modules/expiration/application/expiration-service.ts's
     8	 * createItem) - mission §36 is explicit: "Não usar random key em cada retry".
     9	 */
    10	import { useMutation, type UseMutationOptions, type UseMutationResult } from "@tanstack/react-query";
    11	import { useRef } from "react";
    12	
    13	export interface IdempotentMutationOptions<TData, TVariables> extends Omit<UseMutationOptions<TData, unknown, TVariables>, "mutationFn"> {
    14	  mutationFn: (variables: TVariables, idempotencyKey: string) => Promise<TData>;
    15	  /** When provided, the key survives a full-page reload (e.g. the BFF login redirect during a
    16	   * session interruption mid-submission, mission §49) by persisting it to sessionStorage under
    17	   * this key - never localStorage, this key is submission-scoped, not meant to outlive the tab.
    18	   * Read/write failures (private browsing, disabled storage) degrade to a purely in-memory key
    19	   * rather than throwing - the same "unavailable browser API degrades gracefully" convention as
    20	   * api/client.ts's readCookie. Omitting this option preserves the exact prior in-memory-only
    21	   * behavior. */
    22	  persistenceKey?: string;
    23	}
    24	
    25	export type IdempotentMutationResult<TData, TVariables> = UseMutationResult<TData, unknown, TVariables> & {
    26	  /** Call after a successful submission is fully acknowledged (e.g. the user navigates away
    27	   * or explicitly starts filling a new form) - never call this just to "clear" a failed
    28	   * attempt the user might still retry as the SAME intent. */
    29	  newIntent: () => void;
    30	};
    31	
    32	function readPersistedKey(storageKey: string): string | undefined {
    33	  try {
    34	    return window.sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey) ?? undefined;
    35	  } catch {
    36	    return undefined;
    37	  }
    38	}
    39	
    40	function writePersistedKey(storageKey: string, value: string): void {
    41	  try {
    42	    window.sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, value);
    43	  } catch {
    44	    // Storage unavailable - the key still works, it just won't survive a reload.
    45	  }
    46	}
    47	
    48	export function useIdempotentMutation<TData, TVariables>(options: IdempotentMutationOptions<TData, TVariables>): IdempotentMutationResult<TData, TVariables> {
    49	  const keyRef = useRef<string>();
    50	  if (keyRef.current === undefined) {
    51	    const persisted = options.persistenceKey ? readPersistedKey(options.persistenceKey) : undefined;
    52	    keyRef.current = persisted ?? crypto.randomUUID();
    53	    if (options.persistenceKey && !persisted) {
    54	      writePersistedKey(options.persistenceKey, keyRef.current);
    55	    }
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  const mutation = useMutation<TData, unknown, TVariables>({
    59	    ...options,
    60	    mutationFn: (variables: TVariables) => options.mutationFn(variables, keyRef.current as string),
    61	  });
    62	
    63	  return {
    64	    ...mutation,
    65	    newIntent: () => {
    66	      const fresh = crypto.randomUUID();
    67	      keyRef.current = fresh;
    68	      if (options.persistenceKey) writePersistedKey(options.persistenceKey, fresh);
    69	    },
    70	  };
    71	}

     2	 * G02 (Block 6, D-2xx) — Solicitação de documento (convidado). Deliberately NOT `apiClient`
     3	 * (`api/client.ts`): that client always prefixes `/bff/api` and sends the BFF's OWN CSRF cookie
     4	 * (`__Host-et_csrf`) — the guest surface is a structurally separate, unauthenticated domain
     5	 * (`document-archive-guest-handlers.ts`'s own header comment: "never touches
     6	 * RequestContextResolver/authorize()") with its OWN double-submit CSRF pair
     7	 * (`__Host-et_docarchive_guest_csrf`/`x-csrf-token`), reached via the THREE specific CloudFront
     8	 * behaviors added in this same block (`infra/modules/spa-hosting/main.tf`) — same-origin relative
     9	 * fetches, no cross-origin CORS needed.
    10	 *
    11	 * Every failure this module surfaces collapses to `GuestUnavailableError` — the frontend
    12	 * mirrors the backend's own anti-enumeration discipline (never let a guest distinguish
    13	 * "expired" from "wrong token" from "session gone" from "CSRF mismatch") rather than
    14	 * re-deriving a friendlier message per HTTP status.
    15	 */

    18	export class GuestUnavailableError extends Error {
    19	  constructor() {
    20	    super("Guest access unavailable.");
    21	    this.name = "GuestUnavailableError";
    22	  }
    23	}

    25	const CSRF_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_csrf";
    26	
    27	function readCookie(name: string): string | undefined {
    28	  try {
    29	    const match = document.cookie.split("; ").find((row) => row.startsWith(`${name}=`));
    30	    return match?.split("=")[1];
    31	  } catch {
    32	    return undefined;
    33	  }
    34	}

    36	async function request<T>(path: string, options: { method?: string; body?: unknown; csrf?: boolean } = {}): Promise<T> {
    37	  const method = options.method ?? "GET";
    38	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    39	  if (options.body !== undefined) headers["content-type"] = "application/json";
    40	  if (options.csrf) {
    41	    const token = readCookie(CSRF_COOKIE_NAME);
    42	    if (token) headers["x-csrf-token"] = token;
    43	  }
    44	
    45	  let response: Response;
    46	  try {
    47	    response = await fetch(path, {
    48	      method,
    49	      headers,
    50	      credentials: "include",
    51	      body: options.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined,
    52	    });
    53	  } catch {
    54	    throw new GuestUnavailableError();
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  if (!response.ok) throw new GuestUnavailableError();
    58	
    59	  const text = await response.text();
    60	  return (text ? JSON.parse(text) : undefined) as T;
    61	}

    63	/** `POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/session` — layer 2, the explicit
    64	 * human interstitial that mints a GuestSession AND resolves the credential internally
    65	 * (`GuestDocumentAccessService.startGuestSession`) — G02 never calls the bare layer-1 GET route

# Rodada 2 — Revisão Claude: Armazenamento real de arquivo no caminho guest (G02)

Nota Rodada 1 (Codex, blind): 5,8/10 NEEDS FIXES — 3 achados BLOQUEANTES, 3 MENORES, todos
verificados como reais por leitura direta do código antes desta revisão. Corrige cada um; não
repete o que não mudou (contexto/pesquisa/checklist/fora-de-escopo permanecem os de
`round1-claude-proposal.md`).

## Achados corrigidos

**#1 (BLOQUEANTE) — `DocumentVersion` nasce por `Put`, não por `Update`.** Correto — a versão é
criada em `entries` como `{ Put: buildVersionedCreate(..., version) }`
(`guest-document-access-service.ts:415-435`), o único `Update` da transação é do
`DocumentRequest`. Correção: `fileSetSealed: true, principalFileId, totalFiles: 1,
pendingFileScans: 1` nascem como campos do objeto `version` literal, no MESMO `Put` — nunca um
segundo `Update` no mesmo item (que a `TransactWriteItems` real rejeitaria).

**#2 (BLOQUEANTE) — `DocumentFile` precisa do ponteiro GSI8.** Correto — sem
`documentFileGsi8Keys()`/`deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue()`, o worker de reconciliação nunca
descobre um arquivo PENDING_UPLOAD nunca enviado, e ele fica pendente para sempre em vez de
`TIMEOUT`. Correção: o `Put` do `DocumentFile` guest inclui, EXATAMENTE como `reserveFiles()`
(`document-archive-service.ts:663-693`):
```
const due = deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue({ scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD", createdAt: now })!;
...
...documentFileGsi8Keys({ dueAtIso: due.dueAtIso, tenantId, fileId }),
```

**#3 (BLOQUEANTE) — Gate STARTER/PROMOTER (D-193 item 8/9) já existe e é intencional, não um bug a
corrigir.** Verificado: `isDocumentArchivePromotionEnabled()`
(`src/modules/extraction/application/document-archive-activation.ts`) exige AMBAS
`EXTRACTION_DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_TRIGGER_ENABLED`/`DOCUMENT_ARCHIVE_PROMOTION_ENABLED` — hoje
deliberadamente `false` (`NEXT_SESSION_PROMPT.md` item 4: "ativação é decisão futura reversível").
Enquanto desligadas, **tanto o upload autenticado (A07/A12, já em produção) quanto o guest desta
proposta** ficam com o mesmo comportamento: o arquivo é fisicamente aceito no bucket de quarentena
(S3 PUT bem-sucedido), mas `upload-finalizer-handler.ts`/`malware-result-handler.ts` descartam o
evento físico ANTES de qualquer leitura de `DocumentFile` (`IGNORED_PROMOTION_DISABLED`) — o
arquivo nunca chega a `CLEAN`, nunca fica revisável em A12, e eventualmente expira via
reconciliação (`FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`) como `TIMEOUT`.

**Decisão explícita desta rodada: NÃO ativar essas flags como parte deste design.** A ativação é
uma decisão de produto já nomeada e deliberadamente adiada (item 4 do roadmap), acoplada ao
consumidor de OCR/extração (D-193's "CLEAN sem consumidor" window) — ativá-la unilateralmente aqui
reabriria uma decisão fora do escopo desta tarefa (armazenar bytes do guest), afetando também TODO
upload autenticado já em produção. Esta proposta **herda o mesmo gate já aprovado**, sem
contorná-lo — o guest tem exatamente a mesma paridade de comportamento que o caminho autenticado já
tem hoje, nem mais nem menos permissivo.

**Consequência honesta, registrada explicitamente (não estava em Rodada 1)**: com as flags no
estado atual, esta mudança sozinha AINDA NÃO fecha a propriedade "A14→G02→A13/A12" com um arquivo
de fato revisável — fecha a PARTE que está no orçamento desta decisão (bytes armazenados
corretamente, sob o mesmo mecanismo de segurança/scan já aprovado, nunca fabricando confiança antes
da hora). A ativação do STARTER/PROMOTER continua sendo pendência nomeada separada, cujo
responsável é quem decidir ativar OCR/extração — não esta rodada. A cópia final de G02 (D-264) já
não afirma "arquivo verificado/aceito", só "recebemos seu envio" — continua correta sem mudança,
porque nunca prometeu mais do que isto entrega.

**#4 (MENOR) — validação de formato de `checksumSha256` no serviço.** Corrigido: mesma regra de
`DocumentService.reserveUpload()` (`document-service.ts:85`), portada (não importada
cross-module, mesmo precedente de `storage-quota.ts`):
```
if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(input.checksumSha256)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
```
(nunca `ValidationError` com a mensagem original — colapsa no erro genérico, disciplina
anti-enumeração do módulo).

**#5 (MENOR) — import do frontend errado.** Corrigido: `computeChecksumSha256`/
`uploadDocumentBytes` são exportados por `frontend/src/api/documents.ts` (linhas 34/57), não por
`hooks/useUploadDocument.ts` (que só os importa). G02 importa diretamente de `api/documents.ts`.

**#6 (MENOR) — validação de schema vaza detalhe do Ajv antes do colapso genérico.** Real e mais
sério com os campos novos (range numérico, enum de mediaType, regex de checksum produzem mensagens
mais discriminantes que os 3 campos string atuais). Corrigido, escopo mínimo: em
`handleSubmitEvidence` (`document-archive-guest-handlers.ts:190`), a chamada
`validateAgainstSchema(...)` passa a ser envolvida em try/catch que relança qualquer falha de
schema como `GuestAccessInvalidError` — SÓ nesta rota (as outras rotas guest têm campos
suficientemente pouco discriminantes para não precisar do mesmo tratamento nesta rodada; ver nota
de fora-de-escopo abaixo).

## Fora de escopo (inalterado + 1 item novo)

Tudo de `round1-claude-proposal.md` §"Fora de escopo", MAIS: hardening equivalente do try/catch de
schema nas OUTRAS rotas guest (`handleGetGuestRequest`/`handleListGuestDocumentTypes`) — mesma
classe de problema, mas pré-existente e não introduzida por esta mudança; nomeado aqui para uma
sessão futura, não corrigido agora para não expandir o diff além do gap fechado.

## Design revisado, seção 1 completa (substitui a Rodada 1)

```ts
const due = deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue({ scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD", createdAt: now })!;
const file: DocumentFile = {
  ...documentFileKey(tenantId, documentId, seq, fileId),
  entityType: "DocumentFile",
  tenantId, documentId, versionId, seq: 1,
  fileId, role: "PRINCIPAL", scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD",
  mediaType: input.mediaType, contentLength: input.contentLength, checksumSha256: input.checksumSha256,
  quarantineObject: { bucket: this.quarantineBucket, key: this.buildQuarantineKey(tenantId, documentId, 1, fileId), versionId: "" },
  createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, version: 1,
  ...documentFileGsi8Keys({ dueAtIso: due.dueAtIso, tenantId, fileId }),
};

const version: DocumentVersion = {
  ...documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, 1),
  entityType: "DocumentVersion", versionId, documentId, tenantId, seq: 1,
  state: "RECEIVED", origin: "GUEST_UPLOAD", receivedAt: now,
  fileSetSealed: true, principalFileId: fileId, totalFiles: 1, pendingFileScans: 1, infectedFileScans: 0,
  requestId: resolved.request.documentRequestId,
  createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, version: 1,
  ...reviewQueueGsi5Keys(tenantId, "RECEIVED", now, versionId),
};
```
(`buildQuarantineKey` privado de `document-archive-service.ts` precisa virar exportado/compartilhado —
proposta: mover para `document-file.ts` como função pura, reaproveitada pelos dois call sites, nunca
duplicada.)

Reserva de quota (mesma condição de `reserveFiles()`) entra como entrada adicional na MESMA
`entries[]` já existente da transação. Ordem de checagem no serviço, antes de montar a transação:
mediaType (allowlist) → contentLength (faixa) → checksumSha256 (regex) — qualquer falha lança
`GuestAccessInvalidError` direto, nunca chega a montar a transação.

## Pedido explícito ao Codex nesta rodada

Reavalie a nota considerando as 6 correções acima. Se algum achado bloqueante NÃO estiver
genuinamente fechado por esta revisão, diga exatamente qual e por quê — não aceite a alegação de
"corrigido" sem reconferir contra o código real novamente.

codex
NOTA: 8,4/10  
VEREDITO: NEEDS FIXES

ACHADOS:

1. **RESOLVIDO — criação de `DocumentVersion`.** A revisão agora coloca `fileSetSealed`, `principalFileId`, `totalFiles` e `pendingFileScans` diretamente no `Put` que cria a versão, compatível com o fluxo real de [guest-document-access-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/application/guest-document-access-service.ts:415). Não há mais duas operações sobre o mesmo item na transação.

2. **RESOLVIDO — ponteiro de manutenção GSI8.** O desenho passou a aplicar `deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue()` e `documentFileGsi8Keys()`, reproduzindo corretamente o padrão de `reserveFiles()` em [document-archive-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/application/document-archive-service.ts:663). Isso torna o arquivo abandonado detectável pelo reconciliador e permite a transição para `TIMEOUT`.

3. **RESOLVIDO, COM DEPENDÊNCIA OPERACIONAL EXPLÍCITA — gate STARTER/PROMOTER.** O código confirma que a promoção exige as duas flags em [document-archive-activation.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/extraction/application/document-archive-activation.ts:28), e que finalizador e processador de malware descartam os eventos enquanto o gate estiver desligado. Concordo com não ativar as flags nesta decisão: fazê-lo alteraria também o caminho autenticado e reabriria o rollout de OCR, que é uma decisão separada.

   Isso fecha a pendência específica “guest não armazena bytes”, mas não autoriza declarar fechada a propriedade completa A14→G02→A13/A12. D-264 deve distinguir esses estados: armazenamento guest implementado; jornada revisável ponta a ponta ainda dependente da ativação do promoter. Essa limitação, agora registrada honestamente, não invalida o escopo estreito desta decisão.

4. **RESOLVIDO — validação de checksum no serviço.** A regex proposta corresponde ao precedente real de [document-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document/application/document-service.ts:85), preservando validação em profundidade e erro guest genérico.

5. **RESOLVIDO — importação no frontend.** As funções são realmente exportadas por [documents.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/frontend/src/api/documents.ts:34), e a revisão agora aponta para esse módulo.

6. **RESOLVIDO — detalhe do Ajv na rota alterada.** Encapsular `validateAgainstSchema()` em `handleSubmitEvidence` e converter sua falha em `GuestAccessInvalidError` elimina a diferenciação introduzida pelos novos campos antes da entrada no serviço. O hardening das demais falhas preexistentes pode permanecer separado.

ACHADOS NOVOS:

1. **BLOQUEANTE — “presign fresco” conflita com o deadline imutável do GSI8.** O `DocumentFile` nasce com vencimento fixo em `createdAt + 600s`, conforme [document-file.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/domain/document-file.ts:71). Entretanto, a proposta permite gerar em qualquer replay um novo presign de 600 segundos sem renovar o deadline/GSI8. Um replay próximo ou posterior ao deadline pode receber uma URL ainda válida enquanto o reconciliador muda o arquivo para `TIMEOUT`; o PUT então não poderá avançar para `CLEAN`.

   O desenho precisa escolher uma regra consistente:

   - presignar somente pelo tempo restante do deadline original e falhar genericamente quando ele expirou; ou
   - renovar `createdAt`/deadline/GSI8 mediante atualização OCC transacional antes de emitir o novo presign.

   Além disso, `TIMEOUT` não pode ser tratado como “já recebido, nada a fazer”: ele pode significar precisamente que nenhum byte chegou.

2. **MENOR — as constraints alegadamente reutilizadas não existem no schema de referência.** [docarchive-reserve-files-request.v1.json](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/schemas/api/docarchive-reserve-files-request.v1.json:20) não contém enum de `mediaType` nem máximo de `contentLength`; possui apenas `minLength` e `minimum`. O serviço proposto ainda aplica corretamente allowlist e teto, portanto não é uma falha de segurança, mas a revisão deve especificar explicitamente as novas constraints do schema em vez de dizer que elas serão reutilizadas daquele contrato.

3. **MENOR — pseudocódigo não está mecanicamente autoconsistente.** A seção declarada “completa” usa `seq` sem declará-lo e chama `this.buildQuarantineKey()` no guest service, embora o próprio texto determine mover a função para `document-file.ts`. O desenho arquitetural está claro, mas esses dois pontos devem ser normalizados antes de virar plano direto de implementação.

NOTA: 8,4/10  
VEREDITO: NEEDS FIXES

ACHADOS:

1. **RESOLVIDO — criação de `DocumentVersion`.** A revisão agora coloca `fileSetSealed`, `principalFileId`, `totalFiles` e `pendingFileScans` diretamente no `Put` que cria a versão, compatível com o fluxo real de [guest-document-access-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/application/guest-document-access-service.ts:415). Não há mais duas operações sobre o mesmo item na transação.

2. **RESOLVIDO — ponteiro de manutenção GSI8.** O desenho passou a aplicar `deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue()` e `documentFileGsi8Keys()`, reproduzindo corretamente o padrão de `reserveFiles()` em [document-archive-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/application/document-archive-service.ts:663). Isso torna o arquivo abandonado detectável pelo reconciliador e permite a transição para `TIMEOUT`.

3. **RESOLVIDO, COM DEPENDÊNCIA OPERACIONAL EXPLÍCITA — gate STARTER/PROMOTER.** O código confirma que a promoção exige as duas flags em [document-archive-activation.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/extraction/application/document-archive-activation.ts:28), e que finalizador e processador de malware descartam os eventos enquanto o gate estiver desligado. Concordo com não ativar as flags nesta decisão: fazê-lo alteraria também o caminho autenticado e reabriria o rollout de OCR, que é uma decisão separada.

   Isso fecha a pendência específica “guest não armazena bytes”, mas não autoriza declarar fechada a propriedade completa A14→G02→A13/A12. D-264 deve distinguir esses estados: armazenamento guest implementado; jornada revisável ponta a ponta ainda dependente da ativação do promoter. Essa limitação, agora registrada honestamente, não invalida o escopo estreito desta decisão.

4. **RESOLVIDO — validação de checksum no serviço.** A regex proposta corresponde ao precedente real de [document-service.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document/application/document-service.ts:85), preservando validação em profundidade e erro guest genérico.

5. **RESOLVIDO — importação no frontend.** As funções são realmente exportadas por [documents.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/frontend/src/api/documents.ts:34), e a revisão agora aponta para esse módulo.

6. **RESOLVIDO — detalhe do Ajv na rota alterada.** Encapsular `validateAgainstSchema()` em `handleSubmitEvidence` e converter sua falha em `GuestAccessInvalidError` elimina a diferenciação introduzida pelos novos campos antes da entrada no serviço. O hardening das demais falhas preexistentes pode permanecer separado.

ACHADOS NOVOS:

1. **BLOQUEANTE — “presign fresco” conflita com o deadline imutável do GSI8.** O `DocumentFile` nasce com vencimento fixo em `createdAt + 600s`, conforme [document-file.ts](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/src/modules/document-archive/domain/document-file.ts:71). Entretanto, a proposta permite gerar em qualquer replay um novo presign de 600 segundos sem renovar o deadline/GSI8. Um replay próximo ou posterior ao deadline pode receber uma URL ainda válida enquanto o reconciliador muda o arquivo para `TIMEOUT`; o PUT então não poderá avançar para `CLEAN`.

   O desenho precisa escolher uma regra consistente:

   - presignar somente pelo tempo restante do deadline original e falhar genericamente quando ele expirou; ou
   - renovar `createdAt`/deadline/GSI8 mediante atualização OCC transacional antes de emitir o novo presign.

   Além disso, `TIMEOUT` não pode ser tratado como “já recebido, nada a fazer”: ele pode significar precisamente que nenhum byte chegou.

2. **MENOR — as constraints alegadamente reutilizadas não existem no schema de referência.** [docarchive-reserve-files-request.v1.json](/mnt/c/Users/marcelo.goncalves/Desktop/projects/expiration-tracker/schemas/api/docarchive-reserve-files-request.v1.json:20) não contém enum de `mediaType` nem máximo de `contentLength`; possui apenas `minLength` e `minimum`. O serviço proposto ainda aplica corretamente allowlist e teto, portanto não é uma falha de segurança, mas a revisão deve especificar explicitamente as novas constraints do schema em vez de dizer que elas serão reutilizadas daquele contrato.

3. **MENOR — pseudocódigo não está mecanicamente autoconsistente.** A seção declarada “completa” usa `seq` sem declará-lo e chama `this.buildQuarantineKey()` no guest service, embora o próprio texto determine mover a função para `document-file.ts`. O desenho arquitetural está claro, mas esses dois pontos devem ser normalizados antes de virar plano direto de implementação.
